## Open questions for the Architect (owner rule: implementer makes no interpretive choice)

The committed migration already contains a provisional answer for Q1-Q8, so the build could continue. Each one
must be ruled on, and changed if the ruling differs, before step 1 is complete.

1. **Work window/room per cause.** The spec says "its selected Site/window/room". Provisional: a FindingRevision
   uses `assessment_revision(NEW.assessment_revision_id).window_start/window_end` and `finding.subject_asset_id`. An
   episode revision uses its `assessment_revision_id` window and `cold_episode.asset_id`. Is that the window
   intended, or should it be `window_assessment_revision_id` or the episode bounds?
2. **Source-gate "cold heads".** Provisional: the latest revision per cold episode citing the batch through
   `cold_episode_evidence`, together with the latest cold FindingRevision per Finding citing it through
   `finding_revision_evidence`. Should episodes alone count, or both?
3. **Dirty-key canonical spelling.** Provisional: JSON array of strings, with UUIDs as lowercase hyphenated text
   and instants as `YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ` (second precision, matching `source_envelope.instant_text`). Confirm the
   precision.
4. **`degC_min` null-bound rule.** "Null bounds only when the technical claim is limited/withheld". Provisional
   CHECK: both bounds non-null, OR `state='WITHHELD'`, OR `limitation IS NOT NULL`. Is "limited" meant as
   `limitation IS NOT NULL`?
5. **Integrity extras the spec does not list.** Provisional, all added: a scoped self-FK on `prior_revision_id`, a
   CHECK `(revision_no=1) = (prior_revision_id IS NULL)`, a kind<->model pairing CHECK, `room_asset_id NOT NULL`
   with a scoped `asset` FK (on both revision and work), and nullable `cold_consequence_evidence.reason`
   (mirroring `cold_episode_evidence`). Keep or drop each?
6. **"Server created_at/finished_at".** Provisional: `DEFAULT clock_timestamp()` on revision `created_at`, evidence
   `created_at` (a column the spec did not list), work `enqueued_at` and attempt `finished_at`. Store code is to
   omit these columns. Should the links have `created_at`, and is a DEFAULT enough, or is a trigger that forces
   the server value required?
7. **`source_classification` domain.** Provisional: free nonempty text. Should it be constrained to the S4-05
   values (`AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION|REGISTERED_SIMULATION|...`)? If so, give the exact set.
8. **Failed enqueue.** If the assessment window is empty or the room is missing, the trigger raises and rolls back
   the W2-03 assessment/Finding transaction, which matches 0012's pattern. Confirm that, or say to skip.
9. **Rule-8 "migration trigger ON CONFLICT ... DO NOTHING" pass probe.** `configuration-persistence.ps1` scans only
   `backend/assetops_backend/` (migrations are skipped at line ~786), so a probe appended to a migration would pass
   vacuously. The proposed fix adds a rule-8 text check on `0023_w2_cold_consequence.py`: every
   `INSERT INTO cold_consequence_work` must carry exactly that DO NOTHING arbiter, there must be at least one, and
   any `DO UPDATE` fails. The pass and fail probes would then mutate that migration file. Approve that, or name
   the intended mechanism.
10. **Fail-closed "inserts seen" for the three cold consequence tables.** Requiring at least one consequence-store
    INSERT per table would fail commit 1, because the store methods arrive in commit 2. Proposal: add the probe-name
    and owner fail-closed checks in commit 1 and the inserts-seen check in commit 2. Approve?

# W2-04 code-anchored build specn changing pages.

## Money schema `0023` and rules

Create four new tables (do not widen the mini-grid `financial_consequence_revision`, whose CHECK constrains `POTENTIAL_FUEL_COST`, `fuel-cost-1`, L and priced amount). The Site FK uses `(tenant_id,site_id)` -> `site(tenant_id,id)`; references to FindingRevision, cold episode revision, assessment revision and accepted evidence use composite `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` keys; the source link uses `(tenant_id,site_id,batch_id)`. Add scoped unique targets to referenced tables only where `0022` has not already supplied them. All tables have `tenant_id,site_id`; every immutable row has server `created_at`/`finished_at`, with UPDATE/DELETE refusal triggers. Downgrade refuses while any of these rows exist; otherwise drops only W2-04 triggers/tables/indexes, including its added scoped unique target on the older link table.

* `cold_consequence_revision`: `id uuid PK`, `finding_revision_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `assessment_revision_id` UUID NOT NULL; `revision_no int >0`, `prior_revision_id uuid NULL`; `kind` CHECK `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE|RECORDED_REPAIR`; `state` CHECK `UNPRICED|WITHHELD`; `reason` nonempty; `window_start,window_end timestamptz` with start<end; `room_asset_id uuid`; `overlap_group text`; `quantity_unit` CHECK `degC_min|none`; `quantity_low,quantity_high numeric NULL` nonnegative/ordered; `amount_low,amount_high numeric NULL`, `currency char(3) NULL`; `finding_basis_sha256,consequence_basis_sha256 char(64)`, `model_id` CHECK `cold-exposure/1|cold-repair/1`; `source_classification`, `source_gate_sha256 char(64)`, `assumption_revision_ids jsonb` array, `limitation text NULL`. Unique `(finding_revision_id,kind,revision_no)`, `(finding_revision_id,kind,consequence_basis_sha256)` and scoped `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` for evidence links. DB CHECK requires `amount_low IS NULL AND amount_high IS NULL AND currency IS NULL` for every row; a later approved migration must widen this. `quantity_unit='none'` requires both quantity bounds null; `degC_min` allows null bounds only when the technical claim is limited/withheld. No `RECORDED_REPAIR` row is created for the six replays because no exact accepted cost/work feed exists. `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` may carry nonnegative bounded `degC_min`; if insufficient evidence both bounds null. No zero amount or stock count stands for unknown.
* `cold_consequence_evidence`: `consequence_revision_id,evidence_id,use,reason` with PK `(consequence_revision_id,evidence_id,use)`, `use=SUPPORT|CONTEXT|EXCLUDED` and scoped FKs. Include all exact evidence cited by the pinned Finding/episode basis, no private world keys.
* `cold_consequence_work`: `id uuid PK`, `cause` CHECK `FINDING_REVISION_APPENDED|EPISODE_REVISION_APPENDED|SOURCE_GATE_CHANGED`; nullable `finding_revision_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `publication_batch_id`, with a CHECK requiring exactly the locator for its cause, and scoped FKs; `window_start,window_end` (nonempty, half-open), `room_asset_id`, `dirty_key_sha256 char(64)`, `enqueued_at`; unique `(tenant_id,site_id,dirty_key_sha256)` and `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` for attempts. Add `(tenant_id,site_id,batch_id)` uniqueness to `batch_publication_link` in 0023 for the scoped `publication_batch_id` FK; `batch_id` is that table's actual PK, there is no link `id`. Trigger-only INSERT with `ON CONFLICT (tenant_id,site_id,dirty_key_sha256) DO NOTHING`. A cold FindingRevision or episode revision enqueues its selected Site/window/room; the source-gate trigger enqueues only overlapping heads. No runtime work insert/status update. Backfill existing W2-03 cold heads once during upgrade, idempotently. `0010_financial_assumptions` has a diesel-only DB CHECK as well as a diesel-only publisher: **no `ASSUMPTION_PUBLISHED` cause or trigger in 0023**. A future cold assumption contract must migrate the DB, publisher and dirty-work cause together.
* `cold_consequence_work_attempt`: `id uuid PK`, `work_id` scoped FK, `attempt_no int >0`, `outcome` CHECK `SUCCEEDED|FAILED`, `error_code text NULL`, `consequence_revision_ids jsonb` array, `finished_at`; unique `(work_id,attempt_no)`. Success has no error and exact returned IDs; failure has error and empty IDs. A failed attempt leaves work pending; pending means no successful attempt. Index `(site_id,enqueued_at)` and `(work_id,attempt_no DESC)`.

`overlap_group = 'cold:' + sha256(canonical JSON array [tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id])`: literal lowercase `cold:` prefix, then 64 lowercase hex characters; it is a grouping label, not a V4 event/correlation ID (whose grammar excludes `:`). An excursion and backup Finding of the same selected room/window share it; the financial read groups `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` by this key and takes the single selected excursion exposure basis, never sums both. The start-response Finding may carry a `WITHHELD` context consequence with the same key but zero *counted* exposure entries, not a numeric zero. No cross-window summing without an explicit disjoint-window contract.

The source-gate event is an appended `batch_publication_link` to a previously accepted cold batch, if one exists; its trigger selects only cold heads whose linked accepted IDs use that batch and enqueues `SOURCE_GATE_CHANGED` inside the link transaction. An initial link before acceptance has no heads and creates no money work; later W2-03 Finding/episode append enqueues it. `evidence_publication` is immutable and a new unrelated publication never dirties an existing cold basis. Each trigger derives the same canonical dirty key from `(cause,locator,tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id)` and takes a Site advisory transaction lock before selecting heads, following `0012_evidence_consequence_work.py`'s enqueue lock pattern. The FindingRevision trigger is restricted to `NEW.domain='cold_chain'`. An episode with no warranted Finding still enqueues; the worker records a successful no-op attempt with an empty consequence ID array. No trigger on an accepted disposition bypasses W2-03 assessment: it waits for the new exact cold Finding/episode revision.

`cold-exposure/1` derives observed rectangle degree-minute bounds only from W2-03's persisted episode bounds/accepted evidence, not a continuous curve. A bounded positive or zero technical quantity is allowed only when W2-03 actually proved it; missing-probe/contradictory has null quantity. `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` is unpriced for sound technical bounds because no `cold_stock_quantity`, `cold_stock_unit_value`, reviewed `cold_exposure_rule`, product/currency binding or source-gated valuation exists. These are **reserved future scenario keys**, displayed as missing, not publishable via the diesel-price publisher. Product value/currency and EV tariff would be explicitly authored scenario assumptions and still would not prove sales, collections, spoilage or lost revenue. Recorded repair needs an accepted exact cost/currency/work record; none of the six replay cases has one. Mixed/unknown source, closed S4-05 gate, stale/pending assessment, incomplete period or contradictory evidence yields `WITHHELD` and null amount. LR current-money gate also refuses a current money claim from historical/pending basis. `exact_basis` returns selected Finding/episode/assessment UUIDs, accepted evidence UUIDs, source gate digest, assumption revision UUIDs (empty in W2) and consequence basis SHA. Basis SHA hashes canonical sorted IDs, window/cutoff, model/formula version, quantity bounds, source gate and missing assumption markers; an identical basis returns the existing ID and a changed basis appends a new revision.

`PostgresConsequenceStore` alone inserts `cold_consequence_revision`, `cold_consequence_evidence` and `cold_consequence_work_attempt`; add separate cold methods without changing the existing mini-grid methods. `cold_recompute.process_one` selects unfinished work under `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('cold_consequence:' || work_id,0))`, takes one repeatable-read snapshot, refuses a foreign/missing exact basis, writes consequence/links and `SUCCEEDED` attempt in one transaction. On derivation failure it rolls back that transaction and appends a typed `FAILED` attempt in a fresh transaction. A crash before success commits leaves no partial revision; retries are idempotent. `run` uses the existing `financials/recompute.py:retry_due_at` 1,2,4,8,16-minute schedule and bounded batch; one bad work item cannot starve others. Trigger-created work does not depend on a successful worker.

## Five API reads

`cold_site_api.py:build_cold_site_router` mounts five GET paths below `/api/sites/{site_code}/cold`. Request query for all is optional paired `from,to`, optional `known_at` (strict UTC); Analysis additionally `cursor,limit` (default 50, max 200), matching W2-03's paging spelling. Use W2-03 shared common envelope exactly: `site_id,window:{from,to,known_at},currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since},source_provenance:{classification,source_ids,evidence_ids,basis_sha256},limitations`. Every card/object includes `as_of` and `limitation`; scalar decimals serialize as decimal strings and absent values as JSON null. `currentness.state` is `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`. Valid empty selection is 200 with `UNKNOWN` and empty arrays. Use one selected repeatable-read snapshot for W2-03, source disclosure and consequence heads. No previous consequence becomes a current money claim while a relevant cold work item is queued or failed. Errors use `{error:{code,message}}`; never downgrade 503 into a blank page.

Refusal code contract (same scope and parse semantics as `cold_assessment_api.py:build_cold_assessment_router`): 401 `UNAUTHENTICATED`; 403 `SITE_SCOPE_FORBIDDEN` or `COLD_SOURCE_DISCLOSURE_FORBIDDEN`; 404 `SITE_NOT_FOUND`; 409 `COLD_SELECTION_UNSETTLED` for an explicitly requested revision that has no settled exact basis or `COLD_CURSOR_BASIS_CHANGED` for a changed Analysis basis; 422 `COLD_TIME_INVALID`, `COLD_WINDOW_INVALID`, `COLD_WINDOW_TOO_LONG`, `COLD_PARAMETER_UNKNOWN`, `COLD_LIMIT_INVALID`, or `COLD_CURSOR_INVALID`; 503 `COLD_READ_UNAVAILABLE`. A wrong-type/foreign Site is 404 after access is checked. An absent optional current basis is 200 `UNKNOWN`, not a 409. Analysis cursor is unpadded base64url of sorted, compact UTF-8 JSON `{basis:<64 lowercase hex>,id:<UUID string>,t:<UTC instant>}`; malformed is 422 and an exact basis mismatch is 409. No arbitrary `after` offset or page across a changed basis.

| GET suffix | Exact additional response |
| --- | --- |
| `/overview` | `status:{state:OBSERVED_EXCURSION|NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,claim_level,as_of,limitation}`, `objective:{text,policy_low_deg_c,policy_high_deg_c,room_asset_id,as_of,limitation}`, `temperature_summary:{latest_deg_c,latest_at,coverage:{known_points,expected_points,ratio},episode_count,claim_level,as_of,limitation}`, `backup_summary:{attempts,observed_success,observed_failure,unknown,latest_chain_id,as_of,limitation}`, `causal_strip:[{step:GRID|START_REQUEST|START_RESULT|GENERATOR|TRANSFER|BUS|COMPRESSOR|PROBE,state:OBSERVED|MISSING|CONTRADICTORY,at,evidence_id,as_of,limitation}]`, `findings:{count,items:[{finding_id,revision_id,title,claim_level,link}],as_of,limitation}`, `needs_attention:[{code,text,link,as_of,limitation}]`, `consequence_summary:{state:UNPRICED|WITHHELD|UNKNOWN,quantity:{low,high,unit:'degC_min'|null},amount:{low:null,high:null,currency:null},overlap_group,as_of,limitation}`, `links:{temperature,backup,findings,evidence,analysis,financials}`. The response includes W2-03 selected temperature points only through its linked read, never repackages model truth. |
| `/evidence` | `capabilities:[{claim,state,required_roles,missing_roles,coverage,as_of,limitation}]`, `sources:[{id,role,contact,fact_age,accepted_count,last_accepted_at,as_of,limitation}]`, `links:{analysis,foundation,temperature,backup}`. `contact` is `RECENT|STALE|NEVER|UNKNOWN`; `fact_age` is duration/null, never equipment condition. |
| `/evidence/analysis` | `accepted_records:{items:[{evidence_id,kind,source_time,received_at,quality,role,exclusion,source_id,envelope_sha256,assessment_revision_id,link,as_of,limitation}],next_cursor}`, `claim_rules:[{rule_id,requirement,observed_roles,missing_roles,coverage,result,as_of,limitation}]`, `links:{evidence,findings}`. No private oracle or rejected ordinal masquerades as accepted. |
| `/financials` | `consequences:[{id,kind,state,quantity_bounds:{low,high},quantity_unit,amount_bounds:{low:null,high:null},currency:null,reason,overlap_group,exact_basis,as_of,limitation}]`, `not_included:[{code:STOCK_VALUE_UNDECLARED|EXPOSURE_RULE_UNAPPROVED|SPOILAGE_UNPROVED|REPAIR_COST_ABSENT|SOURCE_GATE_WITHHELD,text,as_of,limitation}]`, `links:{assumptions,findings,evidence}`. Return one counted exposure per overlap group; the optional backup context row is marked nonadditive. |
| `/financial-assumptions` | `items:[{key,revision_id,value,unit,currency,valid_from,valid_to,recorded_at,source,as_of,limitation}]`, `missing_keys:['cold_stock_quantity','cold_stock_unit_value','cold_exposure_rule']` in this slice, `publication_state:'NOT_SUPPORTED_IN_W2'`, `links:{financials}`. `items=[]` for six canonical replays. This is a read-only explicit absence statement, not a publication form. |

Links carry the exact selected `from,to,known_at` and record/revision ID through route builders, URL encoded. The Site overview's Finding count counts distinct current Finding IDs, not episode revisions. `needs_attention` is ordered: pending recompute, probe gap, observed start failure, unknown transfer, missing policy; omit a code only when its predicate is false. No â€œnext actionâ€ link unless an actual W2-03 Finding or Evidence destination exists.

# Role Configuration

Roles are project responsibilities. Agent bindings are configurable defaults.

## Default Bindings

| Role | Default agent | Purpose |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Architect | Codex; scoped Claude exception for the v6.9 site mock-up work on `arch/canonical-v69-pivot` (2026-10-06; see below) | Creates feature maps, causal sequencing, protected seams, and feature-to-task guidance. |
| Planner | Codex (Claude exception ended at the 2026-10-03 21:23 reset; see below) | Converts reviewed feature guidance into small UI-verifiable task definitions. |
| Implementer | Claude | Builds the active task, runs checks, and prepares the review packet. |
| Reviewer | Configurable independent agent; Codex by default (see below) | Reviews work independently from the author. |

## Rules

- Keep role responsibilities stable even if the bound agent changes.
- Do not assume Planner, Architect, or Implementer means a specific vendor unless
  this file says so.
- Implementer may refine implementation steps, but should not redefine feature
  sequencing without Architect or user review.
- Reviewer must be independent from the agent that authored the implementation.
- Claude Code commands in `.claude/commands/` should read this file before
  acting so they can either run the role or prepare a handoff for the configured
  agent.

## Decision Requests Go To The Active Architect

Set by the owner on 2026-10-04 (verbatim in `.agent/owner-T028-closeout-2026-10-04.md`):
"for all decsion requests, let the active architect make them and inform me if
the architect wants me to weigh in as well with its justification and decision".

So the coordinator does not put decision requests to the owner. It sends them
to the Architect bound above, which decides, records the decision with its
justification, and informs the owner. Where the Architect wants the owner to
weigh in, it says so, giving its decision and justification, and the owner may
confirm or overrule. Owner acceptance of a slice (USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED) is not
a decision request and still goes to the owner.

## Architect Was Temporarily Claude, And Is Codex Again

On 2026-09-24 the user set Architect to Claude because Codex had exhausted its
usage limit twice in one day while the task queue waited on an Architect
review. That entry said to revert when capacity allowed. Codex capacity
returned at 18:21 the same day and the binding was reverted at 18:53, so the
exception is closed and the rule below applies again without qualification.

Two Architect passes were produced under the exception, both on
`arch/queue-review-feedback` and both merged: the queue resequencing at
`2970d74` and the hardening-gate removal at `e02d267`. A later reader checking
who authored them should know they were Claude by an explicit, recorded
decision rather than by the coordinator routing around this file.

The lesson worth keeping is the one the exception was written to respect: when
a binding has to change, change it here first and then act. Routing around a
control is worse than the mistake the control prevents.

## Invoking A Codex-Bound Role

A role bound to Codex is run by invoking Codex, not by dispatching the
same-named Claude subagent and merging what it returns. Codex is on PATH and
runs non-interactively:

```
codex exec "<the role brief>"
```

**The obligation is the coordinator's, not the subagent's.** On 2026-09-23 the
Architect correctly flagged three times that it was producing Architect output
while this file bound the role to Codex. Each time the coordinator relayed the
flag to the user as a question and merged the output anyway. A subagent noticing
a binding violation is not a control, because it has already done the work by
the time it can tell anyone. The control is that the coordinator invokes the
bound tool in the first place.

So: before dispatching `assetops-architect` or `assetops-planner`, check the
binding above. If it is Codex, run `codex exec`. The Claude subagents of those
where an independent reviewer has already named the defects and the fix needs
no design choice: prose, docstrings, claim corrections, test assertions,
deletions. The job is confirming the named items closed and nothing else moved.
Fresh rather than resumed, because there is no context worth preserving for
checking named sentences and a clean reader is worth more.

The honest risk, recorded so it is not discovered later: this relies on the
previous review having found everything discoverable in that area. Four
consecutive rounds on T020B each found something the last missed, which is the
argument against cheap re-reviews. **So a Claude verification pass that finds
something unexpected is a signal to escalate that round back to Codex, not a
cheap verdict to accept.** Finding an unnamed defect means the round was
discovery after all and was misclassified.

Two classification traps worth naming, both seen on T020B:

- **A correction round can widen under the Implementer's hand.** The R1a/R1b
  round was three sentences and a deletion when dispatched; it came back having
  also corrected an unnamed fourth sentence and added a property-based guard
  over thirteen windows. Classify on what came back, not on what was sent.
- **"Small" will drift to "everything" if it is not tested against the two
  lists above.** The question is not how many lines changed. It is whether a
  reader had to decide anything.

## Owner Review Routing From S3 Onward (2026-10-11)

Owner (verbatim, `.agent/owner-review-routing-and-merge-2026-10-11.md`): "codex usage optimizaion is important, so keep the same arragment for S2 going forward". For every slice from S3 onward: a fresh Claude `assetops-reviewer` does the slice's first review and every re-review; small written-list fix rounds run on a Sonnet implementer; one batched Codex review covers each milestone's slices together at the milestone's end; Codex Architect/Planner questions are batched rather than sent per question file. This overrides "Codex reviews discovery" below for per-slice first reviews; Codex remains the Architect, the Planner and the milestone reviewer.

## Do Not Write The Review In The Brief

Recorded on 2026-09-26 at the user's instruction, because this project had
been doing the opposite. Reviewer briefs had grown to eight numbered questions,
each naming the thing to judge and supplying the arguments on both sides. That
is not a brief; it is a draft verdict with blanks. A reviewer steered to eight
questions inspects eight things, and the ninth - the one nobody thought to ask
about - is the one that reaches production.

A brief gives the Reviewer:

- the target: branch, commit, spec, packet, and where prior reviews live;
- the standing criteria it is reviewing against, which live in the task file
  and this project's records, not in the brief;
- **what has already been run and verified**, with results, so quota is not
  spent re-running a green suite. This is factual and does not steer;
- the constraints: do not merge, do not fix, where to write, what not to touch.

A brief does **not** give the Reviewer:

- an enumerated list of what to judge;
- the arguments for or against a conclusion;
- which findings the coordinator considers important;
- a named preferred outcome, or a hint of one.

Pointing at an artifact is not steering; summarising what that artifact says is.
Where a genuine question must be asked - a decision only the user can take, a
constraint the Reviewer cannot infer - ask it plainly and separately, and do not
dress an opinion as a question.

The Implementer's packet already states its own uncertainties and residual
risks. That is the right channel for "look at this": the author declaring what
it is unsure of, in a document the Reviewer reads anyway.

## Sort Findings Before Forwarding Them

Recorded on 2026-09-26 after the user asked whether the Reviewer was applying
`D-2026-09-22-milestone-speed-over-purity`. It was; all three T020A1 reviews
cited it, and Codex used it to carry an HTTP-reachable 500, five overflow
sites and several residuals rather than block on them. **The coordinator was
the leak.**

That decision's stopping rule is narrow. Stop only for a defect expensive to
reverse, a claim that would mislead an Implementer, or a decision only the user
can take. **Everything else is recorded and carried.** A Reviewer that rates a
finding low and does not demand it has already applied the rule; forwarding it
as fix work overrides the Reviewer in the direction the decision forbids.

So when a review returns, the coordinator sorts every finding into block or
carry against those three conditions before putting any of it to the user, and
says which is which. A finding that is carried goes to
`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md` with the reason, not into the next round.

T020A1 is the case to remember: five implementation rounds and three review
passes, of which two rounds closed comment counts, a docstring and a Draft
count that had drifted. Each was real. None was a reason to reopen a slice.

## Using Codex Efficiently

Codex quota is **account-level and shared across every model**. On 2026-09-24
it was exhausted twice in one day, the second time mid-review, and switching
to `gpt-6-sol`, `gpt-6-luna`, `gpt-5.6-sol` and `gpt-5.5` all returned the
identical limit with the identical reset time. **You cannot route around an
exhausted limit by changing model.** The only lever is spending less per call,
before the limit is reached.

What it cost that day, measured from the run logs:

| Pass | Tokens | Shell calls | Started |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Reviewer pass 1 | 145,247 | 31 | cold |
| Reviewer pass 2 | 145,568 | 38 | cold |
| Reviewer pass 3 | 25,259, then died | 5 | cold |

Pass 2 spent 145k tokens re-deriving what pass 1 had already established.

### Resume instead of restarting

`codex exec resume <session-id> "<follow-up prompt>"` continues an existing
session with its context intact. `--last` resumes the most recent. The session
id is printed in the header of every run, so **capture it whenever a Codex
role may be returned to** - which is every review, because a review that finds
defects is returned to by definition.

A second pass on the same slice is a follow-up, not a new engagement. Resume
it and say what changed. Start cold only when the subject is genuinely
different or the earlier session would mislead.

**`resume` does not take the same flags as `exec`, and this cost two failed
invocations on 2026-09-25 before it was pinned down.** Two rules:

- Options go **before** the session id: `codex exec resume [OPTIONS]
  <session-id> "<prompt>"`. After it they are parsed as the prompt.
- **`--sandbox` does not exist on `resume`.** Pass it as config instead:
  `-c sandbox_mode="workspace-write"`. `resume` accepts `-c`, `--last`,
  `--all`, `-m`, `--skip-git-repo-check`, `--json`, `-o` and a few others;
  run `codex exec resume --help` rather than assuming a flag carries over.

The working form, verified:

```
codex exec resume -c sandbox_mode="workspace-write" --skip-git-repo-check   <session-id> "<follow-up prompt>" < /dev/null
```

Both failures were argument parsing, rejected before any model call, so they
cost seconds and no quota. The point of recording them is that guidance which
fails on first use is worse than none.

A session resumes across a quota interruption: the third-pass review that died
mid-run at its usage limit was resumed the next day into the same session id
with its context intact.

### Match the model to the work

`-m <model>` selects one. `gpt-6-astra` is the default and the most expensive;
`gpt-6-sol` is the workhorse coding model; `gpt-6-luna` is the cheap one. Full
list: `gpt-6-astra`, `gpt-6-sol`, `gpt-6-luna`, `gpt-5.6-sol`, `gpt-5.6-terra`,
`gpt-5.6-luna`, `gpt-5.5`.

Reserve Astra for genuine architecture and review judgement. Mechanical passes
- a fix round against a written list, a routine Planner expansion, a
consistency sweep - do not need it.

### Never spend a full session on a probe

`~/.codex/config.toml` sets `model_reasoning_effort = "high"` globally, while
the models' own default is `low`. A one-line availability probe therefore runs
at high reasoning. If you must probe, use
`-m gpt-6-luna -c model_reasoning_effort=low`. Better: attempt the real work
and detect the limit from the error, which costs nothing when the limit is
already hit.

### Put verification in the brief, not in the session

Every fact the brief states is work the session does not repeat. Say what you
already ran and what you already verified - test counts, check results,
confirmed line numbers - and say plainly that repeating it is not wanted.
Ask Codex for judgement, not for re-running a green suite.

  anchors, what is next, open risks); the coordinator dispatches a fresh agent
  for the next phase from that file. Full suites and the packet belong to the
  last phase.
- **Hand off at about 120k context.** Set by the owner on 2026-10-02 after
  usage showed 66% of tokens spent above 150k context and 67% in implementer
  subagents. An implementer that nears 120k commits what is safe, writes its
  handoff and stops, even mid-phase; a fresh agent continues from the handoff.
  The coordinator plans phases so that none is expected to reach that size,
  splitting a phase into sub-phases (2a, 2b) rather than letting one run long.
- **Backend tests: focused per phase, full once per slice** (OPS-01,
  `backend/TESTING.md`). Each phase runs the focused and adjacent backend files
  it touched; the real-Postgres full suite runs once per slice, on the final
  revision, with the guide's normal command and JUnit zero-skip check.
  Reviewers (Claude or Codex) cite that same-revision full evidence and run
  focused files; they rerun the full suite only for a finding that needs it.
  Before any full run start `tools/keep-awake-during-tests.ps1` and cap the run
  at twice its expected wall time; a run past the cap is stopped and reported,
  never waited on. One full suite on this machine at a time.
- **Mechanical sub-steps run on Sonnet.** Work fixed by an agreed contract or
  handoff - tests written against a settled interface, an AC-by-AC evidence
  pass, closeout records - is dispatched with `model: sonnet`, like fix rounds.
  Steps that decide a design stay on the session model.

## Token Efficiency Without Lowering Quality (owner, 2026-10-18)

Owner: be very efficient without compromising quality, in every session. Lessons
from W1 rework and W2-00..W2-03 (about 30-40% avoidable rework, then 15-20%):

- **All acceptance rules in the brief from the start.** Layout contract,
  recurring-defect checklist, height rule, plain-language rules, proof list.
  Never introduce a rule mid-build; it forces trimming passes on every slice.
- **Build specs answer the known question classes before coding.** A W2+ build
  spec is ready only when it fixes, against the actual code: identifier
  grammars and formats; exact fixture Site/Foundation values and how bindings
  are published; every seam with path:symbol; guard allowlists the new code
  must satisfy (rule 8 writers, sqlalchemy import allowlist, dependency
  direction); refusal codes; freshness/time references; UI empty/unavailable
  copy and the panel map; how Lab/run projections and stored artifacts look.
  W2-02 needed six question rounds because its first spec skipped these.
  Then the Architect does an **implementer dry-read**: walk the spec commit by
  commit against the real code, write down every question a mid-level
  implementer would have to ask (multiplicity such as per-connector channels,
  which component owns each device, new Foundation keys, readiness codes for
  each missing input, cadence sources, fixture Sites vs revisions, migration
  numbering against in-flight slices), and answer each in the spec. W2-05a
  stopped with eight such questions after the gate alone.
- **Size builder runs to one spec commit each**, with a handoff after each, so
  no run dies mid-commit and the next run reads only the handoff.
- **Model by risk.** First review of a new build and design-deciding work on
  the session model; fix rounds, re-reviews (scoped to prior findings) and
  mechanical steps on Sonnet. A capture- or copy-only fix may be verified by
  the coordinator directly (view the evidence, record a short verify note).
- **Serialize slices that edit the same shared UI files**; parallelize only
  disjoint work (backend vs frontend, different domains).
- **One full suite per round, only when no builder is running.** Do not rerun
  full suites after doc-, test- or CSS-only changes; rerun the touched files.
- **Batch Architect/Planner questions into one Codex call**, and fold a
  preemptive sweep of the slice's remaining commits into that same call.
- **No duplicate agents.** Check for a live agent on a worktree before
  dispatching; never reuse a worktree a reviewer is still using.
- **Mockup drives the design; implementers never decide (owner, 2026-10-18).**
  Every spec maps each mock panel, row, column and chip of each owned screen to
  its data source or an honest gap; no mock element is trimmed or redesigned to
  fit the backend. Any implementer choice the spec did not fix is a defect:
  reviewers flag it Medium (High if user-visible vs the mock), and the Architect
  rules it and amends the spec before merge
  (`.agent/owner-fidelity-and-design-ownership-2026-10-18.md`).
- **Briefs point to files; they do not restate them.** Coordinator replies to
  interim agent notifications stay to one line.

## Updating Bindings

Change bindings here when the team wants a different tool to own a role. Record
material changes in `.ai/DECISIONS.md` if they affect workflow, review
independence, or task ownership.

.ai/DECISIONS.md:3931:For Q1 choose versioned simulated-device quantization, not permanent `INVALID_VALUE` holds or ratio-valued V3. Phase 3 adds a new immutable registered-Kobo publication profile (`registered-kobo-publication` v1) that explicitly declares a 0.000001 source-unit resolution for each numeric device signal and no numeric resolution for ENUM signals. Run setup verifies that each declared numeric unit equals the registered binding's source unit for this profile; a mismatch refuses instead of quantizing in an assumed unit. At the simulated device observation transform, after modeled bias and before `DeviceObservation` is frozen as the public reading, round the exact rational to that quantum using exact round-half-even arithmetic; serialize the result as a canonical finite decimal with insignificant trailing zeros removed. The V3 gateway still only validates and carries the published source-unit value; it never rounds or substitutes kernel truth. The prior `simulator-lab-publication` v3/v4/v5, existing Drafts, frozen observations, old artifacts, sealed envelopes and phase-1 V3/Batch V2 vectors remain unchanged. New Drafts must explicitly select the new profile; freeze its per-signal resolution in the run identity/reporting inputs and prove replay-stable bytes and that the 41 ratio holds become admitted readings without hiding other holds. This resolution is a simulated device property, not a claim about a physical Kobo instrument.

S2-02 then accepts delayed reports through the same authenticated batch path and shows source/published/received times and lag. .ai/DECISIONS.md:3949:Decision: `D-2026-10-09-s2-simulated-publication-clock`. Codex Architect, resolving S2-01c Planner Q1 in `.agent/architect-S2-01c-answers.md`. Select an explicitly advanced, persisted virtual publication clock for each registered Lab run. It is a simulated-device publication timeline, distinct from both the run's world-execution progress and the real UTC clock used for gateway diagnostics/server receipt. A world step may advance the publication clock through its reached simulation instant; after world execution completes, the operator/test harness may advance publication time further without rerunning the kernel. Pause and restart preserve the last advanced instant; neither wall-clock passage nor process restart releases a pending report. Advancing to an earlier instant refuses; repeated advancement to the same instant is idempotent.
.ai/DECISIONS.md:3955:Decision: `D-2026-10-09-s2-gateway-diagnostic-semantics`. Codex Architect, resolving S2-01c phase-1 Q1-Q5 in `.agent/architect-S2-01c-answers-2.md`; this is a contract clarification, not an implementation review. Keep `GatewayDiagnosticsV1` pressure provenance off the V1 wire: `disk_pressure` is the gateway's operative `OK|WARN|STOP` level, while the local read identifies `MEASURED|INJECTED_FAULT_FIXTURE|UNMEASURABLE`. A simulated gateway's central STOP is not measured hardware pressure. At STOP, all new acquisition calls, including replays, refuse before writes or sequence allocation; sealing existing admitted envelopes and emitting diagnostics may continue. A failed free-space probe is locally `UNMEASURABLE` and operatively STOP. The default 60-second cadence and 1 GiB/256 MiB free-space WARN/STOP thresholds are frozen, provisioned demo values, not universal physical safety limits.

Before world completion, explicit advancement cannot pass the world's reached instant, preventing ordinary later samples from acquiring an undeclared publication delay. .ai/DECISIONS.md:4046:Decision: `D-2026-10-09-s2-rotation-reconciliation-followup`. Codex Architect, resolving S2-02 Q37–Q40 and fix re-review R3 in `.agent/architect-pre-S3-answers.md` after S2 closeout; this is a follow-up contract, not a reopened S2 verdict. For the same-account pilot, `ISSUE_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN` may be a supervised operator halt without a local marker; a later successful issue on a readable DB creates a new protected file and revokes earlier selectors atomically. A marker alone is not a trusted second authority. The current fresh *unlocked* no-selector read is **not conclusive proof of rollback**: the original transaction may still finish its COMMIT. Before any non-demo/unattended rotation or real pilot, a bounded S2-02r correction must reconcile under the same gateway-row `FOR UPDATE` lock used by issuance, wait for any prior transaction to finish, then check selector/verifier, credential reference, expected source enablement and survival of the previously active credential in that locked transaction. Only proven non-commit with the old credential active permits deleting the new file; timeout or inconsistent state keeps the protected file and typed unknown outcome. `pg_stat_activity` alone does not establish this ordering. Distinguish a provably pre-transaction failure from an ambiguous COMMIT in that follow-up; the present conservative unknown result is a carry, not secret loss. The v3 store-root-bound comparison correctly closes on older servers until restart; its hash of canonical resolved Evidence/commit roots is accepted, and a moved store needs a fresh report. S2-02r is not an S3 analytic prerequisite.

Preserve the new protected versioned file until a fresh scoped read reconciles its selector, one-way verifier, active/revoked state and expected gateway/source enablement. If the new credential committed, report success and switch the gateway to it; if non-commit and old credential survival are proven, remove only the unissued file; if neither can be proven, return a typed unknown outcome, retain the protected file and halt operator rotation pending reconciliation. Never discard a possibly committed only-usable secret or blindly reissue. A copied legacy-comparison report is not portable to another workspace merely because it points at the same database: bind it to the canonical local Evidence/commit store-root identity as well as tenant, Site UUID, DB identity and current input digest. ## Open questions for the Architect (owner rule: implementer makes no interpretive choice)

The committed migration already contains a provisional answer for Q1-Q8, so the build could continue. Each one
must be ruled on, and changed if the ruling differs, before step 1 is complete.

1. **Work window/room per cause.** The spec says "its selected Site/window/room". Provisional: a FindingRevision
   uses `assessment_revision(NEW.assessment_revision_id).window_start/window_end` and `finding.subject_asset_id`. An
   episode revision uses its `assessment_revision_id` window and `cold_episode.asset_id`. Is that the window
   intended, or should it be `window_assessment_revision_id` or the episode bounds?
2. **Source-gate "cold heads".** Provisional: the latest revision per cold episode citing the batch through
   `cold_episode_evidence`, together with the latest cold FindingRevision per Finding citing it through
   `finding_revision_evidence`. Should episodes alone count, or both?
3. **Dirty-key canonical spelling.** Provisional: JSON array of strings, with UUIDs as lowercase hyphenated text
   and instants as `YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ` (second precision, matching `source_envelope.instant_text`). Confirm the
   precision.
4. **`degC_min` null-bound rule.** "Null bounds only when the technical claim is limited/withheld". Provisional
   CHECK: both bounds non-null, OR `state='WITHHELD'`, OR `limitation IS NOT NULL`. Is "limited" meant as
   `limitation IS NOT NULL`?
5. **Integrity extras the spec does not list.** Provisional, all added: a scoped self-FK on `prior_revision_id`, a
   CHECK `(revision_no=1) = (prior_revision_id IS NULL)`, a kind<->model pairing CHECK, `room_asset_id NOT NULL`
   with a scoped `asset` FK (on both revision and work), and nullable `cold_consequence_evidence.reason`
   (mirroring `cold_episode_evidence`). Keep or drop each?
6. **"Server created_at/finished_at".** Provisional: `DEFAULT clock_timestamp()` on revision `created_at`, evidence
   `created_at` (a column the spec did not list), work `enqueued_at` and attempt `finished_at`. Store code is to
   omit these columns. Should the links have `created_at`, and is a DEFAULT enough, or is a trigger that forces
   the server value required?
7. **`source_classification` domain.** Provisional: free nonempty text. Should it be constrained to the S4-05
   values (`AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION|REGISTERED_SIMULATION|...`)? If so, give the exact set.
8. **Failed enqueue.** If the assessment window is empty or the room is missing, the trigger raises and rolls back
   the W2-03 assessment/Finding transaction, which matches 0012's pattern. Confirm that, or say to skip.
9. **Rule-8 "migration trigger ON CONFLICT ... DO NOTHING" pass probe.** `configuration-persistence.ps1` scans only
   `backend/assetops_backend/` (migrations are skipped at line ~786), so a probe appended to a migration would pass
   vacuously. The proposed fix adds a rule-8 text check on `0023_w2_cold_consequence.py`: every
   `INSERT INTO cold_consequence_work` must carry exactly that DO NOTHING arbiter, there must be at least one, and
   any `DO UPDATE` fails. The pass and fail probes would then mutate that migration file. Approve that, or name
   the intended mechanism.
10. **Fail-closed "inserts seen" for the three cold consequence tables.** Requiring at least one consequence-store
    INSERT per table would fail commit 1, because the store methods arrive in commit 2. Proposal: add the probe-name
    and owner fail-closed checks in commit 1 and the inserts-seen check in commit 2. Approve?

Implementation commits, in order: (1) `0023` schema/triggers/downgrade plus rule-8 policy/probes; (2) pure cold derivation, store and worker with exact basis/replay tests; (3) five selected API reads and typed error/selection tests; (4) five route panels/client, structure tests and 1440/390 captures; (5) final guards and one full backend suite on final revision. Keep each commit reviewable; no W2-04 implementation is included in this planning change.

## Selection, projection and capability

One `ColdSelection` has `tenant_id,site_id,code,from_at,to_at,known_at,watermark,foundation_revision_ids,binding_revision_ids,assessment_revision_id,finding_revision_ids,source_gate`. `from_at` and `to_at` are UTC and half-open; `known_at` defaults to latest committed disposition watermark captured once at request start. Omitted `from,to` selects W2-03's latest settled cold window; exactly one omitted is 422. The effective Foundation/binding is selected at each evidence source time (W2-03), with the selected policy at the requested window; conflicting/absent effective revision is an explicit limitation. 31 days maximum. No newer assessment, Finding or consequence may be silently mixed with an older `known_at`. A late accepted report makes the current projection `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until a new exact assessment and cold consequence both settle. A selected old cutoff is `HISTORICAL`, even when there is no pending work. Determine probe freshness from the latest qualifying accepted probe `source_time` within `[from,to)` compared with `to`: over 10 minutes is `STALE_SOURCE`. Later wall-clock viewing of the 2026-10-06 replay never alone makes it stale or live. Precedence: overlapping queued/failed newer work -> `PENDING_RECOMPUTE`; explicit cutoff older than latest relevant committed disposition -> `HISTORICAL`; no settled selection/probe or future `to` -> `UNKNOWN`; old last probe -> `STALE_SOURCE`; otherwise `CURRENT`. Money can remain `UNPRICED` within a current technical read; pending/old/unresolved money has its own withholding limit.

`ColdSiteReadService` joins, by exact UUIDs, W2-03 `ColdReadService` output, W2-01 Foundation, S4-05 source/publication links and the cold consequence head. It does not use a display-name join or the latest Finding irrespective of window. Capability claims are exactly `TEMPERATURE_POINT,EXCURSION_BOUNDS,BACKUP_START_RESULT,TRANSFER_POSITION,COOLING_RESPONSE,PRODUCT_EXPOSURE`. Each has `state:SUPPORTED|LIMITED|UNAVAILABLE|CONTRADICTORY`, `required_roles:string[]`, `missing_roles:string[]`, `coverage:{known_points:int,expected_points:int,ratio:decimal string}` and `as_of,limitation`. `TEMPERATURE_POINT` requires GOOD calibrated room probe and attested clock; `EXCURSION_BOUNDS` adds policy, two qualifying samples and >=0.8 coverage with no >10-minute bridge; `BACKUP_START_RESULT` requires correlated accepted request/result and the W2-03 causal rules; `TRANSFER_POSITION` needs its own bound transfer report; `COOLING_RESPONSE` needs an accepted compressor state and probe series; `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` means bounded thermal exposure only and requires an eligible excursion. Contradictory accepted facts give `CONTRADICTORY`, not healthy/failure. Source `contact` derives from a diagnostic received no later than selected `known_at` only when that diagnostic actually identifies the source; if only gateway-level contact exists, source contact is `UNKNOWN` with a limitation. `fact_age` is a nonnegative integer number of seconds/null between selected `to` and the last accepted source fact in `[from,to)` at the cutoff; it is distinct from contact. No source contact becomes equipment condition. Panel 3's â€œnext evidenceâ€ is simply a named missing role/link, never a scored recommendation.

Analysis rows are selected accepted public records from the pinned assessment basis and exact links, ordered `(source_time,evidence_id)`, with `evidence_id,kind,source_time,received_at,quality,role,exclusion,source_id,envelope_sha256,assessment_revision_id`; `exclusion` is W2-03's SUPPORT/CONTEXT/EXCLUDED reason or null. Keep a record in the list when excluded, label why, and link to the existing Site Evidence exact record when source gate permits. `claim_rules` lists rule ID `cold-excursion/1` or `cold-start-response/1`, requirement, observed/missing role, coverage and resulting limit, all derived from the selected assessment; no speculative prose rule. Opaque cursor encodes `(source_time,evidence_id)` and selected basis SHA; mismatch returns 409 rather than changing pages.

## Money schema `0023` and rules

Create four new tables (do not widen the mini-grid `financial_consequence_revision`, whose CHECK constrains `POTENTIAL_FUEL_COST`, `fuel-cost-1`, L and priced amount). The Site FK uses `(tenant_id,site_id)` -> `site(tenant_id,id)`; references to FindingRevision, cold episode revision, assessment revision and accepted evidence use composite `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` keys; the source link uses `(tenant_id,site_id,batch_id)`. Add scoped unique targets to referenced tables only where `0022` has not already supplied them. All tables have `tenant_id,site_id`; every immutable row has server `created_at`/`finished_at`, with UPDATE/DELETE refusal triggers. Downgrade refuses while any of these rows exist; otherwise drops only W2-04 triggers/tables/indexes, including its added scoped unique target on the older link table.

* `cold_consequence_revision`: `id uuid PK`, `finding_revision_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `assessment_revision_id` UUID NOT NULL; `revision_no int >0`, `prior_revision_id uuid NULL`; `kind` CHECK `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE|RECORDED_REPAIR`; `state` CHECK `UNPRICED|WITHHELD`; `reason` nonempty; `window_start,window_end timestamptz` with start<end; `room_asset_id uuid`; `overlap_group text`; `quantity_unit` CHECK `degC_min|none`; `quantity_low,quantity_high numeric NULL` nonnegative/ordered; `amount_low,amount_high numeric NULL`, `currency char(3) NULL`; `finding_basis_sha256,consequence_basis_sha256 char(64)`, `model_id` CHECK `cold-exposure/1|cold-repair/1`; `source_classification`, `source_gate_sha256 char(64)`, `assumption_revision_ids jsonb` array, `limitation text NULL`. Unique `(finding_revision_id,kind,revision_no)`, `(finding_revision_id,kind,consequence_basis_sha256)` and scoped `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` for evidence links. DB CHECK requires `amount_low IS NULL AND amount_high IS NULL AND currency IS NULL` for every row; a later approved migration must widen this. `quantity_unit='none'` requires both quantity bounds null; `degC_min` allows null bounds only when the technical claim is limited/withheld. No `RECORDED_REPAIR` row is created for the six replays because no exact accepted cost/work feed exists. `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` may carry nonnegative bounded `degC_min`; if insufficient evidence both bounds null. No zero amount or stock count stands for unknown.
* `cold_consequence_evidence`: `consequence_revision_id,evidence_id,use,reason` with PK `(consequence_revision_id,evidence_id,use)`, `use=SUPPORT|CONTEXT|EXCLUDED` and scoped FKs. Include all exact evidence cited by the pinned Finding/episode basis, no private world keys.
* `cold_consequence_work`: `id uuid PK`, `cause` CHECK `FINDING_REVISION_APPENDED|EPISODE_REVISION_APPENDED|SOURCE_GATE_CHANGED`; nullable `finding_revision_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `publication_batch_id`, with a CHECK requiring exactly the locator for its cause, and scoped FKs; `window_start,window_end` (nonempty, half-open), `room_asset_id`, `dirty_key_sha256 char(64)`, `enqueued_at`; unique `(tenant_id,site_id,dirty_key_sha256)` and `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` for attempts. Add `(tenant_id,site_id,batch_id)` uniqueness to `batch_publication_link` in 0023 for the scoped `publication_batch_id` FK; `batch_id` is that table's actual PK, there is no link `id`. Trigger-only INSERT with `ON CONFLICT (tenant_id,site_id,dirty_key_sha256) DO NOTHING`. A cold FindingRevision or episode revision enqueues its selected Site/window/room; the source-gate trigger enqueues only overlapping heads. No runtime work insert/status update. Backfill existing W2-03 cold heads once during upgrade, idempotently. `0010_financial_assumptions` has a diesel-only DB CHECK as well as a diesel-only publisher: **no `ASSUMPTION_PUBLISHED` cause or trigger in 0023**. A future cold assumption contract must migrate the DB, publisher and dirty-work cause together.
* `cold_consequence_work_attempt`: `id uuid PK`, `work_id` scoped FK, `attempt_no int >0`, `outcome` CHECK `SUCCEEDED|FAILED`, `error_code text NULL`, `consequence_revision_ids jsonb` array, `finished_at`; unique `(work_id,attempt_no)`. Success has no error and exact returned IDs; failure has error and empty IDs. A failed attempt leaves work pending; pending means no successful attempt. Index `(site_id,enqueued_at)` and `(work_id,attempt_no DESC)`.

`overlap_group = 'cold:' + sha256(canonical JSON array [tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id])`: literal lowercase `cold:` prefix, then 64 lowercase hex characters; it is a grouping label, not a V4 event/correlation ID (whose grammar excludes `:`). An excursion and backup Finding of the same selected room/window share it; the financial read groups `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` by this key and takes the single selected excursion exposure basis, never sums both. The start-response Finding may carry a `WITHHELD` context consequence with the same key but zero *counted* exposure entries, not a numeric zero. No cross-window summing without an explicit disjoint-window contract.

The source-gate event is an appended `batch_publication_link` to a previously accepted cold batch, if one exists; its trigger selects only cold heads whose linked accepted IDs use that batch and enqueues `SOURCE_GATE_CHANGED` inside the link transaction. An initial link before acceptance has no heads and creates no money work; later W2-03 Finding/episode append enqueues it. `evidence_publication` is immutable and a new unrelated publication never dirties an existing cold basis. Each trigger derives the same canonical dirty key from `(cause,locator,tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id)` and takes a Site advisory transaction lock before selecting heads, following `0012_evidence_consequence_work.py`'s enqueue lock pattern. The FindingRevision trigger is restricted to `NEW.domain='cold_chain'`. An episode with no warranted Finding still enqueues; the worker records a successful no-op attempt with an empty consequence ID array. No trigger on an accepted disposition bypasses W2-03 assessment: it waits for the new exact cold Finding/episode revision.

`cold-exposure/1` derives observed rectangle degree-minute bounds only from W2-03's persisted episode bounds/accepted evidence, not a continuous curve. A bounded positive or zero technical quantity is allowed only when W2-03 actually proved it; missing-probe/contradictory has null quantity. `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` is unpriced for sound technical bounds because no `cold_stock_quantity`, `cold_stock_unit_value`, reviewed `cold_exposure_rule`, product/currency binding or source-gated valuation exists. These are **reserved future scenario keys**, displayed as missing, not publishable via the diesel-price publisher. Product value/currency and EV tariff would be explicitly authored scenario assumptions and still would not prove sales, collections, spoilage or lost revenue. Recorded repair needs an accepted exact cost/currency/work record; none of the six replay cases has one. Mixed/unknown source, closed S4-05 gate, stale/pending assessment, incomplete period or contradictory evidence yields `WITHHELD` and null amount. LR current-money gate also refuses a current money claim from historical/pending basis. `exact_basis` returns selected Finding/episode/assessment UUIDs, accepted evidence UUIDs, source gate digest, assumption revision UUIDs (empty in W2) and consequence basis SHA. Basis SHA hashes canonical sorted IDs, window/cutoff, model/formula version, quantity bounds, source gate and missing assumption markers; an identical basis returns the existing ID and a changed basis appends a new revision.

`PostgresConsequenceStore` alone inserts `cold_consequence_revision`, `cold_consequence_evidence` and `cold_consequence_work_attempt`; add separate cold methods without changing the existing mini-grid methods. `cold_recompute.process_one` selects unfinished work under `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('cold_consequence:' || work_id,0))`, takes one repeatable-read snapshot, refuses a foreign/missing exact basis, writes consequence/links and `SUCCEEDED` attempt in one transaction. On derivation failure it rolls back that transaction and appends a typed `FAILED` attempt in a fresh transaction. A crash before success commits leaves no partial revision; retries are idempotent. `run` uses the existing `financials/recompute.py:retry_due_at` 1,2,4,8,16-minute schedule and bounded batch; one bad work item cannot starve others. Trigger-created work does not depend on a successful worker.

## Five API reads

`cold_site_api.py:build_cold_site_router` mounts five GET paths below `/api/sites/{site_code}/cold`. Request query for all is optional paired `from,to`, optional `known_at` (strict UTC); Analysis additionally `cursor,limit` (default 50, max 200), matching W2-03's paging spelling. Use W2-03 shared common envelope exactly: `site_id,window:{from,to,known_at},currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since},source_provenance:{classification,source_ids,evidence_ids,basis_sha256},limitations`. Every card/object includes `as_of` and `limitation`; scalar decimals serialize as decimal strings and absent values as JSON null. `currentness.state` is `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`. Valid empty selection is 200 with `UNKNOWN` and empty arrays. Use one selected repeatable-read snapshot for W2-03, source disclosure and consequence heads. No previous consequence becomes a current money claim while a relevant cold work item is queued or failed. Errors use `{error:{code,message}}`; never downgrade 503 into a blank page.

Refusal code contract (same scope and parse semantics as `cold_assessment_api.py:build_cold_assessment_router`): 401 `UNAUTHENTICATED`; 403 `SITE_SCOPE_FORBIDDEN` or `COLD_SOURCE_DISCLOSURE_FORBIDDEN`; 404 `SITE_NOT_FOUND`; 409 `COLD_SELECTION_UNSETTLED` for an explicitly requested revision that has no settled exact basis or `COLD_CURSOR_BASIS_CHANGED` for a changed Analysis basis; 422 `COLD_TIME_INVALID`, `COLD_WINDOW_INVALID`, `COLD_WINDOW_TOO_LONG`, `COLD_PARAMETER_UNKNOWN`, `COLD_LIMIT_INVALID`, or `COLD_CURSOR_INVALID`; 503 `COLD_READ_UNAVAILABLE`. A wrong-type/foreign Site is 404 after access is checked. An absent optional current basis is 200 `UNKNOWN`, not a 409. Analysis cursor is unpadded base64url of sorted, compact UTF-8 JSON `{basis:<64 lowercase hex>,id:<UUID string>,t:<UTC instant>}`; malformed is 422 and an exact basis mismatch is 409. No arbitrary `after` offset or page across a changed basis.

| GET suffix | Exact additional response |
| --- | --- |
| `/overview` | `status:{state:OBSERVED_EXCURSION|NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,claim_level,as_of,limitation}`, `objective:{text,policy_low_deg_c,policy_high_deg_c,room_asset_id,as_of,limitation}`, `temperature_summary:{latest_deg_c,latest_at,coverage:{known_points,expected_points,ratio},episode_count,claim_level,as_of,limitation}`, `backup_summary:{attempts,observed_success,observed_failure,unknown,latest_chain_id,as_of,limitation}`, `causal_strip:[{step:GRID|START_REQUEST|START_RESULT|GENERATOR|TRANSFER|BUS|COMPRESSOR|PROBE,state:OBSERVED|MISSING|CONTRADICTORY,at,evidence_id,as_of,limitation}]`, `findings:{count,items:[{finding_id,revision_id,title,claim_level,link}],as_of,limitation}`, `needs_attention:[{code,text,link,as_of,limitation}]`, `consequence_summary:{state:UNPRICED|WITHHELD|UNKNOWN,quantity:{low,high,unit:'degC_min'|null},amount:{low:null,high:null,currency:null},overlap_group,as_of,limitation}`, `links:{temperature,backup,findings,evidence,analysis,financials}`. The response includes W2-03 selected temperature points only through its linked read, never repackages model truth. |
| `/evidence` | `capabilities:[{claim,state,required_roles,missing_roles,coverage,as_of,limitation}]`, `sources:[{id,role,contact,fact_age,accepted_count,last_accepted_at,as_of,limitation}]`, `links:{analysis,foundation,temperature,backup}`. `contact` is `RECENT|STALE|NEVER|UNKNOWN`; `fact_age` is duration/null, never equipment condition. |
| `/evidence/analysis` | `accepted_records:{items:[{evidence_id,kind,source_time,received_at,quality,role,exclusion,source_id,envelope_sha256,assessment_revision_id,link,as_of,limitation}],next_cursor}`, `claim_rules:[{rule_id,requirement,observed_roles,missing_roles,coverage,result,as_of,limitation}]`, `links:{evidence,findings}`. No private oracle or rejected ordinal masquerades as accepted. |
| `/financials` | `consequences:[{id,kind,state,quantity_bounds:{low,high},quantity_unit,amount_bounds:{low:null,high:null},currency:null,reason,overlap_group,exact_basis,as_of,limitation}]`, `not_included:[{code:STOCK_VALUE_UNDECLARED|EXPOSURE_RULE_UNAPPROVED|SPOILAGE_UNPROVED|REPAIR_COST_ABSENT|SOURCE_GATE_WITHHELD,text,as_of,limitation}]`, `links:{assumptions,findings,evidence}`. Return one counted exposure per overlap group; the optional backup context row is marked nonadditive. |
| `/financial-assumptions` | `items:[{key,revision_id,value,unit,currency,valid_from,valid_to,recorded_at,source,as_of,limitation}]`, `missing_keys:['cold_stock_quantity','cold_stock_unit_value','cold_exposure_rule']` in this slice, `publication_state:'NOT_SUPPORTED_IN_W2'`, `links:{financials}`. `items=[]` for six canonical replays. This is a read-only explicit absence statement, not a publication form. |

Links carry the exact selected `from,to,known_at` and record/revision ID through route builders, URL encoded. The Site overview's Finding count counts distinct current Finding IDs, not episode revisions. `needs_attention` is ordered: pending recompute, probe gap, observed start failure, unknown transfer, missing policy; omit a code only when its predicate is false. No â€œnext actionâ€ link unless an actual W2-03 Finding or Evidence destination exists.

## v69 panel contract and UI

The title source is `frontend/docs/architecture/v69-rendered-panels.json`; use `expectV69Panels` from the existing W1/W2 route test helper and assert the indexed titles **at render time**. The table gives the exact slot and field, with an in-panel gap if unavailable. No extra leading answer card. Preserve mock relative widths, density and visual grammar from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, `mockups.css`, and shared W1-F1 CSS.

| Route ID | Panel â†’ exact content and gap |
| --- | --- |
| `cold` | 1 **What happened Â· power â†’ cooling â†’ product conditions** â†’ `status,objective,backup_summary,causal_strip`; unknown step says which link is unobserved. 2 **Chamber temperature** â†’ selected `/temperature.points,policy,gaps` and `temperature_summary`; gap is an empty sampled chart, not a flat line. 3 **Needs attention** â†’ `needs_attention,findings.items`; W5 Action/Incident/Maintenance has no record. 4 **Bounded money consequence** â†’ `consequence_summary`; `Unpriced product exposure`, null amount. 5 **Next step** â†’ exact Finding/Evidence links or `No recorded next step`. |

Use `frontend/src/sites/__tests__/coldFoundation.test.tsx` as the current route/`expectV69Panels` pattern; W2-03 cold route tests supply its exact window selection pattern. Every valid named route keeps panel skeletons in unavailable/refused/pending states. At 390 px the Analysis and exact-record links remain accessible. Capture every route at 1440 and 390 beside the same mock screen; log app URL, mock hash, fixture, Site, UTC window/cutoff, revision and image paths. Compare top fold, panel widths/heights, grid, typography, color, spacing, density and mobile flow. Use `.agent/architect-W1-fidelity.md` recurring-defect checklist; a panel >~1.3Ã— mock height needs correction or documented evidence-based content reason. Structural mismatch is High.

Exact in-panel copy on valid empty selection: `No cold assessment for this window` in cold panels 1-3; `No accepted records for this window` in Analysis panel 1; `Claim limit not assessed` in Analysis panel 2; `No assumption in use` and `Cold stock and exposure assumptions have not been declared` in the money/assumption panels; `No recorded next step` in cold panel 5. Pending: `New evidence is waiting to be assessed` alongside the last selected cutoff, without a current money value. Historical: `Earlier evidence cutoff` alongside the selected cutoff. Stale: `Latest usable probe is more than 10 minutes before this window ended`; never say the gateway or equipment is offline. A 403 source disclosure keeps the five route panel skeletons and shows `Source details are not available for this Site` inside affected panels, with no leaked IDs. A 503 read shows `Cold Site data is temporarily unavailable` inside all panels, with a retry control; it is not a successful empty read. Preserve each route's exact titles and in-panel gaps through these states. The assumptions panel remains read-only even when the backend returns 200 `items=[]`; do not send the existing diesel assumption POST from a cold screen.

## Fixtures, named tests and guards

Use `contract-fixtures/w2/cold/{normal,excursion,backup-failed,missing-probe,late-reading,contradictory}.json` (`cold-replay-manifest/1`) through registered Lab, Stage, gateway Batch V2 and ordinary S2 acceptance; never seed accepted rows. The fixture Site is `COLD-LAB-001` (`COLDCHAIN`, simulated source), imported from `backend/tests/fixtures/w2/cold-lab-001.yaml`: `room-1`, `probe-1`, `ambient-1`, `compressor-1`, `transfer-1`, `generator-1`, `bus-1`, `grid-1`; door forcing `door-1` resolves to the room sensor. """W2-04 commit 1: immutable, unpriced cold consequence history and trigger-only dirty work.

`.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md` "Money schema `0023` and rules" (decision D-2026-10-18-w2-04-buildspec). Four new
tenant/Site-scoped, append-only tables; the mini-grid `financial_*` tables (0010-0012) and their rows are untouched.

- `cold_consequence_revision`: one immutable consequence revision per (FindingRevision, kind, basis). Every amount and
  currency is NULL by DB CHECK: W2 has no approved cold stock value, currency or exposure valuation rule. A later
  approved migration must widen that CHECK, the diesel-only assumption publisher and the dirty-work causes together.
- `cold_consequence_evidence`: the exact accepted evidence a revision cites, with its SUPPORT/CONTEXT/EXCLUDED use.
- `cold_consequence_work`: dirty work inserted only by the triggers below, never by runtime code. A cold
  FindingRevision (`NEW.domain = 'cold_chain'`) or a cold episode revision enqueues its Site/assessment window/room;
  an appended `batch_publication_link` enqueues `SOURCE_GATE_CHANGED` only for cold heads whose linked accepted
  evidence came from that batch. Each trigger takes the Site advisory transaction lock before selecting heads and
  inserts through `cold_consequence_enqueue` with `ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, site_id, dirty_key_sha256) DO NOTHING`.
  The dirty key is SHA-256 of the canonical JSON array `[cause, locator, tenant_id, site_id, window_start,
  window_end, room_asset_id]` (sorted-key, no-whitespace JSON; lowercase UUIDs; UTC `Z` instants to the second).
  There is no `ASSUMPTION_PUBLISHED` cause: 0010 is diesel-only in DB and publisher.
- `cold_consequence_work_attempt`: SUCCEEDED (no error, exact revision IDs) or FAILED (typed error, no IDs) attempts.

Upgrade backfills the existing W2-03 cold heads (latest cold FindingRevision per Finding, latest revision per cold
episode) once, idempotently. `batch_publication_link` gains a scoped `(tenant_id, site_id, batch_id)` unique target
for the work FK. Downgrade refuses while any W2-04 row exists; otherwise it drops only W2-04 objects.

Revision ID: 0023_w2_cold_consequence
Revises: 0022_w2_cold_assessment
Create Date: 2026-10-18

COLD_CONSEQUENCE_TABLES = ("cold_consequence_revision", "cold_consequence_evidence", "cold_consequence_work",
