# One expression, used verbatim by every enqueue path (a test compares the stored bodies).
ENQUEUE_LOCK = "PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('cold_consequence_enqueue:' || p_site_id::text, 0));"

SCHEMA = r"""
ALTER TABLE batch_publication_link
    ADD CONSTRAINT batch_publication_link_scope_unique UNIQUE (tenant_id, site_id, batch_id);

CREATE TABLE cold_consequence_revision (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id uuid NOT NULL,
    site_id uuid NOT NULL,
    finding_revision_id uuid NOT NULL,
    episode_revision_id uuid NOT NULL,
    assessment_revision_id uuid NOT NULL,
    revision_no integer NOT NULL CHECK (revision_no > 0),
    prior_revision_id uuid NULL,
    kind text NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('PRODUCT_EXPOSURE', 'RECORDED_REPAIR')),
    state text NOT NULL CHECK (state IN ('UNPRICED', 'WITHHELD')),
    reason text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(reason)) > 0),
    window_start timestamptz NOT NULL,
    window_end timestamptz NOT NULL,
    room_asset_id uuid NOT NULL,
    overlap_group text NOT NULL CHECK (overlap_group ~ '^cold:[0-9a-f]{64}$'),
    quantity_unit text NOT NULL CHECK (quantity_unit IN ('degC_min', 'none')),
    quantity_low numeric NULL,
    quantity_high numeric NULL,
    amount_low numeric NULL,
    amount_high numeric NULL,
    currency char(3) NULL,
    finding_basis_sha256 char(64) NOT NULL CHECK (finding_basis_sha256 ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'),
    consequence_basis_sha256 char(64) NOT NULL CHECK (consequence_basis_sha256 ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'),
    model_id text NOT NULL CHECK (model_id IN ('cold-exposure/1', 'cold-repair/1')),
    source_classification text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(source_classification)) > 0),
    source_gate_sha256 char(64) NOT NULL CHECK (source_gate_sha256 ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'),
    assumption_revision_ids jsonb NOT NULL,
    limitation text NULL,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT clock_timestamp(),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_revision_site_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id) REFERENCES site(tenant_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_revision_finding_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, finding_revision_id)
        REFERENCES finding_revision(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_revision_episode_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, episode_revision_id)
        REFERENCES cold_episode_revision(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_revision_assessment_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, assessment_revision_id)
        REFERENCES assessment_revision(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_revision_room_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, room_asset_id)
        REFERENCES asset(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_revision_prior_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, prior_revision_id)
        REFERENCES cold_consequence_revision(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_revision_no_unique UNIQUE (finding_revision_id, kind, revision_no),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_revision_basis_unique UNIQUE (finding_revision_id, kind, consequence_basis_sha256),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_revision_scope_unique UNIQUE (tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_revision_prior_chain CHECK ((revision_no = 1) = (prior_revision_id IS NULL)),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_revision_kind_model CHECK (
        (kind = 'PRODUCT_EXPOSURE' AND model_id = 'cold-exposure/1')
        OR (kind = 'RECORDED_REPAIR' AND model_id = 'cold-repair/1')),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_revision_window CHECK (window_start < window_end),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_revision_quantity CHECK (
        (quantity_low IS NULL OR quantity_low >= 0) AND (quantity_high IS NULL OR quantity_high >= 0)
        AND (quantity_low IS NULL OR quantity_high IS NULL OR quantity_low <= quantity_high)),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_revision_unit_none CHECK (
        quantity_unit <> 'none' OR (quantity_low IS NULL AND quantity_high IS NULL)),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_revision_null_bound_limited CHECK (
        quantity_unit <> 'degC_min' OR (quantity_low IS NOT NULL AND quantity_high IS NOT NULL)
        OR state = 'WITHHELD' OR limitation IS NOT NULL),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_revision_unpriced CHECK (
        amount_low IS NULL AND amount_high IS NULL AND currency IS NULL),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_revision_assumptions_array CHECK (jsonb_typeof(assumption_revision_ids) = 'array')
);
CREATE INDEX cold_consequence_revision_site_window
    ON cold_consequence_revision (site_id, window_start, window_end, room_asset_id);

CREATE TABLE cold_consequence_evidence (
    consequence_revision_id uuid NOT NULL,
    tenant_id uuid NOT NULL,
    site_id uuid NOT NULL,
    evidence_id uuid NOT NULL,
    use text NOT NULL CHECK (use IN ('SUPPORT', 'CONTEXT', 'EXCLUDED')),
    reason text NULL,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT clock_timestamp(),
    PRIMARY KEY (consequence_revision_id, evidence_id, use),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_evidence_revision_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, consequence_revision_id)
        REFERENCES cold_consequence_revision(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_evidence_evidence_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, evidence_id)
        REFERENCES accepted_evidence(tenant_id, site_id, id)
);

CREATE TABLE cold_consequence_work (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    tenant_id uuid NOT NULL,
    site_id uuid NOT NULL,
    cause text NOT NULL CHECK (cause IN ('FINDING_REVISION_APPENDED', 'EPISODE_REVISION_APPENDED',
                                         'SOURCE_GATE_CHANGED')),
    finding_revision_id uuid NULL,
    episode_revision_id uuid NULL,
    publication_batch_id uuid NULL,
    window_start timestamptz NOT NULL,
    window_end timestamptz NOT NULL,
    room_asset_id uuid NOT NULL,
    dirty_key_sha256 char(64) NOT NULL CHECK (dirty_key_sha256 ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'),
    enqueued_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT clock_timestamp(),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_work_site_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id) REFERENCES site(tenant_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_work_finding_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, finding_revision_id)
        REFERENCES finding_revision(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_work_episode_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, episode_revision_id)
        REFERENCES cold_episode_revision(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_work_batch_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, publication_batch_id)
        REFERENCES batch_publication_link(tenant_id, site_id, batch_id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_work_room_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, room_asset_id)
        REFERENCES asset(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_work_cause_shape CHECK (
        (cause = 'FINDING_REVISION_APPENDED' AND finding_revision_id IS NOT NULL
            AND episode_revision_id IS NULL AND publication_batch_id IS NULL)
        OR (cause = 'EPISODE_REVISION_APPENDED' AND episode_revision_id IS NOT NULL
            AND finding_revision_id IS NULL AND publication_batch_id IS NULL)
        OR (cause = 'SOURCE_GATE_CHANGED' AND publication_batch_id IS NOT NULL
            AND finding_revision_id IS NULL AND episode_revision_id IS NULL)),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_work_window CHECK (window_start < window_end),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_work_dirty_unique UNIQUE (tenant_id, site_id, dirty_key_sha256),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_work_scope_unique UNIQUE (tenant_id, site_id, id)
);
CREATE INDEX cold_consequence_work_site_enqueued ON cold_consequence_work (site_id, enqueued_at);

CREATE TABLE cold_consequence_work_attempt (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    tenant_id uuid NOT NULL,
    site_id uuid NOT NULL,
    work_id uuid NOT NULL,
    attempt_no integer NOT NULL CHECK (attempt_no > 0),
    outcome text NOT NULL CHECK (outcome IN ('SUCCEEDED', 'FAILED')),
    error_code text NULL,
    consequence_revision_ids jsonb NOT NULL,
    finished_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT clock_timestamp(),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_attempt_work_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, work_id)
        REFERENCES cold_consequence_work(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_attempt_no_unique UNIQUE (work_id, attempt_no),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_attempt_ids_array CHECK (jsonb_typeof(consequence_revision_ids) = 'array'),
    CONSTRAINT cold_consequence_attempt_outcome_shape CHECK (
        (outcome = 'SUCCEEDED' AND error_code IS NULL)
        OR (outcome = 'FAILED' AND error_code IS NOT NULL AND length(btrim(error_code)) > 0
            AND consequence_revision_ids = '[]'::jsonb))
);
CREATE INDEX cold_consequence_attempt_latest ON cold_consequence_work_attempt (work_id, attempt_no DESC);
"""

APPEND_ONLY = "".join(
    f"CREATE TRIGGER {t}_append_only BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON {t} "
    "FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION ledger_append_only();\n"
    for t in COLD_CONSEQUENCE_TABLES)

TRIGGERS = rf"""
CREATE FUNCTION cold_consequence_dirty_key(p_cause text, p_locator uuid, p_tenant_id uuid, p_site_id uuid,
                                           p_window_start timestamptz, p_window_end timestamptz,
                                           p_room_asset_id uuid) RETURNS char(64)
LANGUAGE sql STABLE STRICT AS $$
    SELECT encode(sha256(convert_to('[' || concat_ws(',',
        to_json(p_cause)::text,
        to_json(p_locator::text)::text,
        to_json(p_tenant_id::text)::text,
        to_json(p_site_id::text)::text,
        to_json(to_char(p_window_start AT TIME ZONE 'UTC', 'YYYY-MM-DD"T"HH24:MI:SS"Z"'))::text,
        to_json(to_char(p_window_end AT TIME ZONE 'UTC', 'YYYY-MM-DD"T"HH24:MI:SS"Z"'))::text,
        to_json(p_room_asset_id::text)::text) || ']', 'UTF8')), 'hex')
$$;

-- The one runtime-free INSERT into cold_consequence_work. Callers hold the Site enqueue lock.
CREATE FUNCTION cold_consequence_enqueue(p_cause text, p_locator uuid, p_tenant_id uuid, p_site_id uuid,
                                         p_window_start timestamptz, p_window_end timestamptz,
                                         p_room_asset_id uuid) RETURNS void
LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
    INSERT INTO cold_consequence_work (tenant_id, site_id, cause, finding_revision_id, episode_revision_id,
                                       publication_batch_id, window_start, window_end, room_asset_id,
                                       dirty_key_sha256)
    VALUES (p_tenant_id, p_site_id, p_cause,
            CASE WHEN p_cause = 'FINDING_REVISION_APPENDED' THEN p_locator END,
            CASE WHEN p_cause = 'EPISODE_REVISION_APPENDED' THEN p_locator END,
            CASE WHEN p_cause = 'SOURCE_GATE_CHANGED' THEN p_locator END,
            p_window_start, p_window_end, p_room_asset_id,
            cold_consequence_dirty_key(p_cause, p_locator, p_tenant_id, p_site_id, p_window_start, p_window_end,
                                       p_room_asset_id))
    ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, site_id, dirty_key_sha256) DO NOTHING;
END;
$$;

-- A cold FindingRevision enqueues its Site, its primary assessment window and its Finding's subject room.
CREATE FUNCTION cold_consequence_dirty_on_finding_revision() RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE
    p_site_id uuid := NEW.site_id;
    w record;
BEGIN
    {ENQUEUE_LOCK}
    SELECT a.window_start, a.window_end, f.subject_asset_id AS room INTO STRICT w
    FROM assessment_revision a, finding f
    WHERE a.tenant_id = NEW.tenant_id AND a.site_id = NEW.site_id AND a.id = NEW.assessment_revision_id
      AND f.tenant_id = NEW.tenant_id AND f.site_id = NEW.site_id AND f.id = NEW.finding_id;
    PERFORM cold_consequence_enqueue('FINDING_REVISION_APPENDED', NEW.id, NEW.tenant_id, NEW.site_id,
                                     w.window_start, w.window_end, w.room);
    RETURN NULL;
END;
$$;
CREATE TRIGGER cold_consequence_dirty_on_finding_revision AFTER INSERT ON finding_revision
    FOR EACH ROW WHEN (NEW.domain = 'cold_chain')
    EXECUTE FUNCTION cold_consequence_dirty_on_finding_revision();

-- A cold episode revision (warranted Finding or not) enqueues its Site, assessment window and episode room.
CREATE FUNCTION cold_consequence_dirty_on_episode_revision() RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE
    p_site_id uuid := NEW.site_id;
    w record;
BEGIN
    {ENQUEUE_LOCK}
    SELECT a.window_start, a.window_end, e.asset_id AS room INTO STRICT w
    FROM assessment_revision a, cold_episode e
    WHERE a.tenant_id = NEW.tenant_id AND a.site_id = NEW.site_id AND a.id = NEW.assessment_revision_id
      AND e.tenant_id = NEW.tenant_id AND e.site_id = NEW.site_id AND e.id = NEW.episode_id;
    PERFORM cold_consequence_enqueue('EPISODE_REVISION_APPENDED', NEW.id, NEW.tenant_id, NEW.site_id,
                                     w.window_start, w.window_end, w.room);
    RETURN NULL;
END;
$$;
CREATE TRIGGER cold_consequence_dirty_on_episode_revision AFTER INSERT ON cold_episode_revision
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION cold_consequence_dirty_on_episode_revision();

-- An appended source-publication link changes the source gate of the cold heads (latest episode revision per
-- episode, latest cold FindingRevision per Finding) whose linked accepted evidence came from that batch; only
-- those heads' Site/window/room are enqueued. A link before any acceptance finds no heads and enqueues nothing.
CREATE FUNCTION cold_consequence_dirty_on_source_gate() RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE
    p_site_id uuid := NEW.site_id;
    h record;
BEGIN
    {ENQUEUE_LOCK}
    FOR h IN
        SELECT DISTINCT a.window_start, a.window_end, e.asset_id AS room
        FROM cold_episode_revision r
        JOIN cold_episode e ON e.tenant_id = r.tenant_id AND e.site_id = r.site_id AND e.id = r.episode_id
        JOIN assessment_revision a ON a.tenant_id = r.tenant_id AND a.site_id = r.site_id
            AND a.id = r.assessment_revision_id
        WHERE r.tenant_id = NEW.tenant_id AND r.site_id = NEW.site_id
          AND r.revision_no = (SELECT max(x.revision_no) FROM cold_episode_revision x
                               WHERE x.episode_id = r.episode_id)
          AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM cold_episode_evidence l
                      JOIN accepted_evidence ev ON ev.tenant_id = l.tenant_id AND ev.site_id = l.site_id
                          AND ev.id = l.evidence_id
                      WHERE l.episode_revision_id = r.id AND ev.batch_id = NEW.batch_id)
        UNION
        SELECT DISTINCT a.window_start, a.window_end, f.subject_asset_id AS room
        FROM finding_revision r
        JOIN finding f ON f.tenant_id = r.tenant_id AND f.site_id = r.site_id AND f.id = r.finding_id
        JOIN assessment_revision a ON a.tenant_id = r.tenant_id AND a.site_id = r.site_id
            AND a.id = r.assessment_revision_id
        WHERE r.tenant_id = NEW.tenant_id AND r.site_id = NEW.site_id AND r.domain = 'cold_chain'
          AND r.revision_no = (SELECT max(x.revision_no) FROM finding_revision x
                               WHERE x.finding_id = r.finding_id)
          AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM finding_revision_evidence l
                      JOIN accepted_evidence ev ON ev.tenant_id = l.tenant_id AND ev.site_id = l.site_id
                          AND ev.id = l.evidence_id
                      WHERE l.finding_revision_id = r.id AND ev.batch_id = NEW.batch_id)
        ORDER BY 1, 2, 3
    LOOP
        PERFORM cold_consequence_enqueue('SOURCE_GATE_CHANGED', NEW.batch_id, NEW.tenant_id, NEW.site_id,
                                         h.window_start, h.window_end, h.room);
    END LOOP;
    RETURN NULL;
END;
$$;
CREATE TRIGGER cold_consequence_dirty_on_source_gate AFTER INSERT ON batch_publication_link
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION cold_consequence_dirty_on_source_gate();
"""

# Existing W2-03 cold heads are enqueued once; ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING makes a repeat a no-op.
BACKFILL = r"""
SELECT cold_consequence_enqueue('FINDING_REVISION_APPENDED', r.id, r.tenant_id, r.site_id, a.window_start,
                                a.window_end, f.subject_asset_id)
FROM finding_revision r
JOIN finding f ON f.tenant_id = r.tenant_id AND f.site_id = r.site_id AND f.id = r.finding_id
JOIN assessment_revision a ON a.tenant_id = r.tenant_id AND a.site_id = r.site_id AND a.id = r.assessment_revision_id
WHERE r.domain = 'cold_chain'
  AND r.revision_no = (SELECT max(x.revision_no) FROM finding_revision x WHERE x.finding_id = r.finding_id)
ORDER BY r.site_id, r.id;

SELECT cold_consequence_enqueue('EPISODE_REVISION_APPENDED', r.id, r.tenant_id, r.site_id, a.window_start,
                                a.window_end, e.asset_id)
FROM cold_episode_revision r
JOIN cold_episode e ON e.tenant_id = r.tenant_id AND e.site_id = r.site_id AND e.id = r.episode_id
JOIN assessment_revision a ON a.tenant_id = r.tenant_id AND a.site_id = r.site_id AND a.id = r.assessment_revision_id
WHERE r.revision_no = (SELECT max(x.revision_no) FROM cold_episode_revision x WHERE x.episode_id = r.episode_id)
ORDER BY r.site_id, r.id;
"""

REFUSE_IF_USED = r"""
DO $$
BEGIN
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM cold_consequence_revision) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM cold_consequence_evidence)
       OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM cold_consequence_work) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM cold_consequence_work_attempt) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Downgrade of 0023_w2_cold_consequence refused: cold consequence revisions, evidence links, work or attempts exist. 0022 cannot represent them, and no row is deleted to force a downgrade.';
    END IF;
END;
$$;
"""

DOWNGRADE = """
DROP TRIGGER cold_consequence_dirty_on_source_gate ON batch_publication_link;
DROP TRIGGER cold_consequence_dirty_on_episode_revision ON cold_episode_revision;
DROP TRIGGER cold_consequence_dirty_on_finding_revision ON finding_revision;
DROP FUNCTION cold_consequence_dirty_on_source_gate();
DROP FUNCTION cold_consequence_dirty_on_episode_revision();
DROP FUNCTION cold_consequence_dirty_on_finding_revision();
DROP TABLE cold_consequence_work_attempt;
DROP TABLE cold_consequence_work;
DROP TABLE cold_consequence_evidence;
DROP TABLE cold_consequence_revision;
DROP FUNCTION cold_consequence_enqueue(text, uuid, uuid, uuid, timestamptz, timestamptz, uuid);
DROP FUNCTION cold_consequence_dirty_key(text, uuid, uuid, uuid, timestamptz, timestamptz, uuid);
ALTER TABLE batch_publication_link DROP CONSTRAINT batch_publication_link_scope_unique;
"""


def upgrade() -> None:
    op.execute(SCHEMA)
    op.execute(APPEND_ONLY)
    op.execute(TRIGGERS)
    op.execute(BACKFILL)


def downgrade() -> None:
    op.execute(REFUSE_IF_USED)
    op.execute(DOWNGRADE)

Owner: be very efficient without compromising quality, in every session. Lessons
from W1 rework and W2-00..W2-03 (about 30-40% avoidable rework, then 15-20%):

- **All acceptance rules in the brief from the start.** Layout contract,
  recurring-defect checklist, height rule, plain-language rules, proof list.
  Never introduce a rule mid-build; it forces trimming passes on every slice.
- **Build specs answer the known question classes before coding.** A W2+ build
  spec is ready only when it fixes, against the actual code: identifier
  grammars and formats; exact fixture Site/Foundation values and how bindings
  are published; every seam with path:symbol; guard allowlists the new code
  must satisfy (rule 8 writers, sqlalchemy import allowlist, dependency
  direction); refusal codes; freshness/time references; UI empty/unavailable
  copy and the panel map; how Lab/run projections and stored artifacts look.
  W2-02 needed six question rounds because its first spec skipped these.
  Then the Architect does an **implementer dry-read**: walk the spec commit by
  commit against the real code, write down every question a mid-level
  implementer would have to ask (multiplicity such as per-connector channels,
  which component owns each device, new Foundation keys, readiness codes for
  each missing input, cadence sources, fixture Sites vs revisions, migration
  numbering against in-flight slices), and answer each in the spec. W2-05a
  stopped with eight such questions after the gate alone.
- **Size builder runs to one spec commit each**, with a handoff after each, so
  no run dies mid-commit and the next run reads only the handoff.
- **Model by risk.** First review of a new build and design-deciding work on
  the session model; fix rounds, re-reviews (scoped to prior findings) and
  mechanical steps on Sonnet. A capture- or copy-only fix may be verified by
  the coordinator directly (view the evidence, record a short verify note).
- **Serialize slices that edit the same shared UI files**; parallelize only
  disjoint work (backend vs frontend, different domains).
- **One full suite per round, only when no builder is running.** Do not rerun
  full suites after doc-, test- or CSS-only changes; rerun the touched files.

Unique `(episode_id,revision_no)` and `(episode_id,basis_sha256)`; scoped FKs to episode and assessment revision. Check each earliest/latest pair ordered when both present, low/high numeric pairs nonnegative and ordered when both present, JSON array shape, and peak null if no usable point. Index `(site_id,episode_id,revision_no DESC)`. Install append-only UPDATE/DELETE refusal triggers on all three; no upsert update.

I’m making those rules explicit in the spec and mapping the mock’s status, cards, rows, chips and links.

+## Final Architect rulings on commit 1 (Q1-Q10)
+
+This section overrides shorthand above where the two differ. Migration `0023` on `task/W2-04-cold-site-evidence-money` is provisional until its listed corrections are made; passing migration tests do not waive them.
+
+1. **Q1 CHANGE — selected window and room.** Keep `assessment_revision.window_start/window_end` for each trigger; neither episode bounds nor `window_assessment_revision_id` is a money window. For an `EXCURSION`, `cold_episode.asset_id` is the room. For a `START_RESPONSE`, that asset and the start Finding's `subject_asset_id` are the **generator**, so they must never fill `room_asset_id` or the overlap key. Resolve the effective chamber room from the exact assessment's selected `cold-roles-1` Foundation/binding basis (the same room that its temperature policy names); require one scoped room, or raise and roll back. Apply this to the Finding trigger, episode trigger, source-gate trigger and backfill. Prove that excursion and start Finding for one assessment produce the same room/window/group and that no generator ID is stored as a room.
+2. **Q2 ACCEPT.** Source-gate link dirties latest cold episode heads citing the batch through `cold_episode_evidence` and latest cold Finding heads citing it through `finding_revision_evidence`. Scope every subquery by tenant and Site as well as parent ID; distinct heads may produce distinct work rows but identical selected consequence basis returns the same revision.
+3. **Q3 CHANGE — preserve instant precision.** Canonical JSON array of strings and lowercase hyphenated UUIDs stand. Use UTC `Z` timestamps with six fractional digits **when microseconds are nonzero**, and whole seconds otherwise, matching canonical source instant formatting. Reject precision beyond microseconds at the API boundary; never truncate a selected window to seconds when hashing. The six replay windows happen to have whole seconds. SQL and Python golden vectors must match for whole-second and fractional inputs.
+4. **Q4 ACCEPT with meaning.** `limitation IS NOT NULL` means a named technical limit, not an arbitrary note. For an open W2-03 excursion with lower `degC_min` and null upper, keep `UNPRICED`, store that partial bound and a nonempty `OPEN_EPISODE_UPPER_UNKNOWN` limitation. `WITHHELD` may have either/both null bounds. A sound closed exposure has both. Null amount/currency remains mandatory in every case.
+5. **Q5 ACCEPT each extra.** Keep scoped self-FK, first-revision/prior-link CHECK, kind/model pairing, nonnull scoped room FK on revision and work, and nullable evidence-link reason. Add a same-Finding/kind predecessor check in store logic; FK alone does not prove chain membership. A successful no-Finding episode gets a no-op attempt, not a fabricated Finding or consequence.
+6. **Q6 ACCEPT.** The listed `DEFAULT clock_timestamp()` columns, including evidence-link `created_at`, are server times. The sole store writer must omit them, and tests must assert they lie in the DB transaction's observed interval. Do not accept client timestamps in store methods. A forcing trigger is unnecessary for this owner-restricted append-only table.
+7. **Q7 CHANGE — constrain the aggregate.** `source_classification` is exactly `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION|REGISTERED_SIMULATION|MIXED|UNKNOWN`, the values returned by `evidence/source_provenance.py`; add a SQL CHECK. `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` is not a current class. Preserve reason/detail and exact IDs separately; `UNKNOWN` and `MIXED` withhold money.
+8. **Q8 ACCEPT.** Missing room or invalid assessment window is an invariant violation: raise and roll back the W2-03 assessment/Finding transaction, following `0012` rather than silently dropping dirty work. The worker reports `FAILED` for later derivation faults; it cannot repair a missing enqueue.
+9. **Q9 ACCEPT with a nonvacuous guard.** Rule 8 scans runtime modules, so inspect the exact `0023` migration separately. Require at least one `INSERT INTO cold_consequence_work`, and require every such INSERT to use `ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, site_id, dirty_key_sha256) DO NOTHING`; reject `DO UPDATE`, another arbiter or a missing INSERT. Probe by mutating a temporary copy of the migration and restoring it, with pass and fail cases; no probe may pass solely because migrations are skipped by the runtime scan.
+10. **Q10 ACCEPT staged enforcement.** Commit 1 installs table/owner/probe-name registration and refuses foreign and mutating writes. Commit 2 adds real store INSERTs and enables per-table `inserts seen` assertions for revision, evidence and attempt. Before commit 2 is declared done, all three must be nonvacuously seen; no empty/fake SQL is allowed to satisfy the guard. The trigger-only work check is active in commit 1.
+
+## Implementer dry-read: exact backend behavior
+
+| Question a builder would otherwise decide | Binding answer and proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| Which Finding gets an exposure revision? | Only a selected, warranted `EXCURSION` FindingRevision with an exact `claim.episode_revision_id` and eligible episode. `START_RESPONSE` is chronology/context; do not emit the optional context consequence in W2-04. A successful start, unsupported excursion, or missing-probe episode without a Finding produces no consequence and may close its trigger work with an empty-ID success attempt. This avoids a second counted exposure and an invented zero row. |
+| Which assessment is exact? | Join FindingRevision `assessment_revision_id`, `claim.episode_revision_id`, episode revision `assessment_revision_id`, and the selected W2-03 assessment pair by scoped IDs. A mismatch is a typed worker failure `COLD_BASIS_MISMATCH`, never a latest-row fallback. For a source-gate event, re-read the latest settled heads at its transaction snapshot and enqueue only heads whose exact evidence IDs use the linked batch. |
+| How is `degC_min` computed? | Copy the selected excursion revision's `degree_minutes_low/high` as decimal strings after checking policy, coverage and evidence eligibility. Do not integrate an interpolated chart, derive from duration alone or use private trajectory. Preserve a proved lower bound with unknown upper as in Q4. `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED` has no exposure row; no episode means `UNKNOWN` summary if the selection is otherwise incomplete. |
+| How does revision identity work? | Hash the sorted exact accepted IDs with their SUPPORT/CONTEXT/EXCLUDED uses and reasons, Finding/episode/assessment UUIDs and basis hashes, selected window/cutoff, `cold-exposure/1`, quantity bounds, aggregate source class/gate digest, and explicit missing cold assumption markers. Existing identical `(finding_revision_id,kind,basis_sha256)` returns its ID. A changed basis appends `revision_no=max+1` and `prior_revision_id` under the work advisory lock. Never overwrite; reversion to a previously used basis must remain audit-visible, so include the newly selected cutoff/revision ID in the hash. |
+| What exactly is pending and when does a work item finish? | Unfinished means no `SUCCEEDED` attempt. Include any relevant PENDING/LEASED/FAILED W2-03 cold assessment job and any cold consequence work with no success; a failed attempt does not clear pending. Relevant means same tenant/Site, overlapping half-open selected window and room. Select work in `(enqueued_at,id)` order, lock its UUID, and use `retry_due_at` from the last attempt; no row status UPDATE. An episode-only no-op success has `[]` IDs. |
+| Which source/cutoff gates apply? | Resolve S4-05 aggregate provenance from the exact evidence IDs, not Site mode or a Lab manifest. Recompute after a new publication link. Return historical technical rows at an older explicit `known_at` with the historical label; never call their amount current. Closed disclosure gives 403 before returning restricted accepted IDs; mixed/unknown classification and pending/stale currentness withhold a current money claim. No cold price exists even with coherent simulation. |
+| What does `RECORDED_REPAIR` mean here? | It is schema reserved only. Neither a W2-03 start Finding nor mock WR-019 is an accepted, costed work record. No `RECORDED_REPAIR` row, repair amount, currency, incident or maintenance item is synthesized in W2-04. |
+| How does the API keep one snapshot? | Authenticate and scope Site first. Open one repeatable-read transaction; capture latest committed S2 disposition watermark once, resolve paired window and optional `known_at`, then call W2-03 read/store, Foundation, source provenance and consequence read methods with the same connection. If W2-03 exposes only HTTP calls, add a caller-connection read method to its existing assessment adapter; never invoke loopback HTTP for composition or import a SQL driver into `cold_site_reads.py`. |
+| What is a valid explicit selection/cursor? | `from,to,known_at` are strict UTC instants; both window bounds or neither, `from<to`, at most 31 days, no unknown query keys. `known_at` cannot exceed the captured committed watermark for claims; a future cutoff is 422 `COLD_TIME_INVALID`. Cursor stores exact canonical selection/basis and `(source_time,evidence_id)`; a well-formed cursor for another selection or changed basis is 409. Page after the tuple, never by offset. Empty selected evidence is 200 `UNKNOWN`; unavailable/corrupt storage is 503, not empty. |
+| What do `as_of` and contact mean? | For an accepted record, `as_of=received_at` (with separate `source_time`); for assessment/episode/Finding, use immutable revision `created_at`; for consequence use its `created_at`; for a configured policy use effective revision time; for a missing value use selected `known_at` and a limitation. Source contact uses only an identified source-specific diagnostic at/before cutoff; otherwise `UNKNOWN`, even if a gateway is online. Accepted fact age is `to - latest qualifying source_time`, separate from contact. |
+| Where do exact links go? | Only emit a clickable target after an integration test proves the target route renders that exact ID with Site, window and cutoff preserved. For an unproved cold record or assessment target, display the exact ID as labelled text plus `Analysis view not available for this cold record`; Finding and Site Evidence links that pass their own route proof remain clickable. Never turn a mock Action, Incident, Maintenance or unbuilt Cooling destination into a link. |
+
+Use `cold_site_reads.py` as a pure composition service, and put SQL in the existing assessment/consequence adapters. `financials/cold_recompute.py` receives store protocols and no SQL driver. The five GETs are composed in `main.py`; do not change the mini-grid routers. Run architecture/dependency, workflow, writer guard and targeted import probes after each owning commit. The review packet states the exact migration SHA, W2-03 predecessor revision, replay manifest IDs, accepted IDs, selected revision IDs and test database name.

+## Final Architect rulings on commit 1 (Q1-Q10)
+
+This section overrides shorthand above where the two differ. Migration `0023` on `task/W2-04-cold-site-evidence-money` is provisional until its listed corrections are made; passing migration tests do not waive them.
+
+1. **Q1 CHANGE — selected window and room.** Keep `assessment_revision.window_start/window_end` for each trigger; neither episode bounds nor `window_assessment_revision_id` is a money window. For an `EXCURSION`, `cold_episode.asset_id` is the room. For a `START_RESPONSE`, that asset and the start Finding's `subject_asset_id` are the **generator**, so they must never fill `room_asset_id` or the overlap key. Resolve the effective chamber room from the exact assessment's selected `cold-roles-1` Foundation/binding basis (the same room that its temperature policy names); require one scoped room, or raise and roll back. Apply this to the Finding trigger, episode trigger, source-gate trigger and backfill. Prove that excursion and start Finding for one assessment produce the same room/window/group and that no generator ID is stored as a room.
+2. **Q2 ACCEPT.** Source-gate link dirties latest cold episode heads citing the batch through `cold_episode_evidence` and latest cold Finding heads citing it through `finding_revision_evidence`. Scope every subquery by tenant and Site as well as parent ID; distinct heads may produce distinct work rows but identical selected consequence basis returns the same revision.
+3. **Q3 CHANGE — preserve instant precision.** Canonical JSON array of strings and lowercase hyphenated UUIDs stand. Use UTC `Z` timestamps with six fractional digits **when microseconds are nonzero**, and whole seconds otherwise, matching canonical source instant formatting. Reject precision beyond microseconds at the API boundary; never truncate a selected window to seconds when hashing. The six replay windows happen to have whole seconds. SQL and Python golden vectors must match for whole-second and fractional inputs.
+4. **Q4 ACCEPT with meaning.** `limitation IS NOT NULL` means a named technical limit, not an arbitrary note. For an open W2-03 excursion with lower `degC_min` and null upper, keep `UNPRICED`, store that partial bound and a nonempty `OPEN_EPISODE_UPPER_UNKNOWN` limitation. `WITHHELD` may have either/both null bounds. A sound closed exposure has both. Null amount/currency remains mandatory in every case.
+5. **Q5 ACCEPT each extra.** Keep scoped self-FK, first-revision/prior-link CHECK, kind/model pairing, nonnull scoped room FK on revision and work, and nullable evidence-link reason. Add a same-Finding/kind predecessor check in store logic; FK alone does not prove chain membership. A successful no-Finding episode gets a no-op attempt, not a fabricated Finding or consequence.
+6. **Q6 ACCEPT.** The listed `DEFAULT clock_timestamp()` columns, including evidence-link `created_at`, are server times. The sole store writer must omit them, and tests must assert they lie in the DB transaction's observed interval. Do not accept client timestamps in store methods. A forcing trigger is unnecessary for this owner-restricted append-only table.
+7. **Q7 CHANGE — constrain the aggregate.** `source_classification` is exactly `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION|REGISTERED_SIMULATION|MIXED|UNKNOWN`, the values returned by `evidence/source_provenance.py`; add a SQL CHECK. `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` is not a current class. Preserve reason/detail and exact IDs separately; `UNKNOWN` and `MIXED` withhold money.
+8. **Q8 ACCEPT.** Missing room or invalid assessment window is an invariant violation: raise and roll back the W2-03 assessment/Finding transaction, following `0012` rather than silently dropping dirty work. The worker reports `FAILED` for later derivation faults; it cannot repair a missing enqueue.
+9. **Q9 ACCEPT with a nonvacuous guard.** Rule 8 scans runtime modules, so inspect the exact `0023` migration separately. Require at least one `INSERT INTO cold_consequence_work`, and require every such INSERT to use `ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, site_id, dirty_key_sha256) DO NOTHING`; reject `DO UPDATE`, another arbiter or a missing INSERT. Probe by mutating a temporary copy of the migration and restoring it, with pass and fail cases; no probe may pass solely because migrations are skipped by the runtime scan.
+10. **Q10 ACCEPT staged enforcement.** Commit 1 installs table/owner/probe-name registration and refuses foreign and mutating writes. Commit 2 adds real store INSERTs and enables per-table `inserts seen` assertions for revision, evidence and attempt. Before commit 2 is declared done, all three must be nonvacuously seen; no empty/fake SQL is allowed to satisfy the guard. The trigger-only work check is active in commit 1.
+
+## Implementer dry-read: exact backend behavior
+
+| Question a builder would otherwise decide | Binding answer and proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| Which Finding gets an exposure revision? | Only a selected, warranted `EXCURSION` FindingRevision with an exact `claim.episode_revision_id` and eligible episode. `START_RESPONSE` is chronology/context; do not emit the optional context consequence in W2-04. A successful start, unsupported excursion, or missing-probe episode without a Finding produces no consequence and may close its trigger work with an empty-ID success attempt. This avoids a second counted exposure and an invented zero row. |
+| Which assessment is exact? | Join FindingRevision `assessment_revision_id`, `claim.episode_revision_id`, episode revision `assessment_revision_id`, and the selected W2-03 assessment pair by scoped IDs. A mismatch is a typed worker failure `COLD_BASIS_MISMATCH`, never a latest-row fallback. For a source-gate event, re-read the latest settled heads at its transaction snapshot and enqueue only heads whose exact evidence IDs use the linked batch. |
+| How is `degC_min` computed? | Copy the selected excursion revision's `degree_minutes_low/high` as decimal strings after checking policy, coverage and evidence eligibility. Do not integrate an interpolated chart, derive from duration alone or use private trajectory. Preserve a proved lower bound with unknown upper as in Q4. `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED` has no exposure row; no episode means `UNKNOWN` summary if the selection is otherwise incomplete. |
+| How does revision identity work? | Hash the sorted exact accepted IDs with their SUPPORT/CONTEXT/EXCLUDED uses and reasons, Finding/episode/assessment UUIDs and basis hashes, selected window/cutoff, `cold-exposure/1`, quantity bounds, aggregate source class/gate digest, and explicit missing cold assumption markers. Existing identical `(finding_revision_id,kind,basis_sha256)` returns its ID. A changed basis appends `revision_no=max+1` and `prior_revision_id` under the work advisory lock. Never overwrite; reversion to a previously used basis must remain audit-visible, so include the newly selected cutoff/revision ID in the hash. |
+| What exactly is pending and when does a work item finish? | Unfinished means no `SUCCEEDED` attempt. Include any relevant PENDING/LEASED/FAILED W2-03 cold assessment job and any cold consequence work with no success; a failed attempt does not clear pending. Relevant means same tenant/Site, overlapping half-open selected window and room. Select work in `(enqueued_at,id)` order, lock its UUID, and use `retry_due_at` from the last attempt; no row status UPDATE. An episode-only no-op success has `[]` IDs. |
+| Which source/cutoff gates apply? | Resolve S4-05 aggregate provenance from the exact evidence IDs, not Site mode or a Lab manifest. Recompute after a new publication link. Return historical technical rows at an older explicit `known_at` with the historical label; never call their amount current. Closed disclosure gives 403 before returning restricted accepted IDs; mixed/unknown classification and pending/stale currentness withhold a current money claim. No cold price exists even with coherent simulation. |
+| What does `RECORDED_REPAIR` mean here? | It is schema reserved only. Neither a W2-03 start Finding nor mock WR-019 is an accepted, costed work record. No `RECORDED_REPAIR` row, repair amount, currency, incident or maintenance item is synthesized in W2-04. |
+| How does the API keep one snapshot? | Authenticate and scope Site first. Open one repeatable-read transaction; capture latest committed S2 disposition watermark once, resolve paired window and optional `known_at`, then call W2-03 read/store, Foundation, source provenance and consequence read methods with the same connection. If W2-03 exposes only HTTP calls, add a caller-connection read method to its existing assessment adapter; never invoke loopback HTTP for composition or import a SQL driver into `cold_site_reads.py`. |
+| What is a valid explicit selection/cursor? | `from,to,known_at` are strict UTC instants; both window bounds or neither, `from<to`, at most 31 days, no unknown query keys. `known_at` cannot exceed the captured committed watermark for claims; a future cutoff is 422 `COLD_TIME_INVALID`. Cursor stores exact canonical selection/basis and `(source_time,evidence_id)`; a well-formed cursor for another selection or changed basis is 409. Page after the tuple, never by offset. Empty selected evidence is 200 `UNKNOWN`; unavailable/corrupt storage is 503, not empty. |
+| What do `as_of` and contact mean? | For an accepted record, `as_of=received_at` (with separate `source_time`); for assessment/episode/Finding, use immutable revision `created_at`; for consequence use its `created_at`; for a configured policy use effective revision time; for a missing value use selected `known_at` and a limitation. Source contact uses only an identified source-specific diagnostic at/before cutoff; otherwise `UNKNOWN`, even if a gateway is online. Accepted fact age is `to - latest qualifying source_time`, separate from contact. |
+| Where do exact links go? | Only emit a clickable target after an integration test proves the target route renders that exact ID with Site, window and cutoff preserved. For an unproved cold record or assessment target, display the exact ID as labelled text plus `Analysis view not available for this cold record`; Finding and Site Evidence links that pass their own route proof remain clickable. Never turn a mock Action, Incident, Maintenance or unbuilt Cooling destination into a link. |
+
+Use `cold_site_reads.py` as a pure composition service, and put SQL in the existing assessment/consequence adapters. `financials/cold_recompute.py` receives store protocols and no SQL driver. The five GETs are composed in `main.py`; do not change the mini-grid routers. Run architecture/dependency, workflow, writer guard and targeted import probes after each owning commit. The review packet states the exact migration SHA, W2-03 predecessor revision, replay manifest IDs, accepted IDs, selected revision IDs and test database name.

+| Route and mock slot | Exact source or honest W2-04 treatment |
+| --- | --- |

Financial bridge preserves selection. Next step names the first observed missing role or exact Finding/Evidence link. Mock's witnessed test and ACT-051 are not asserted without a managed Action. |
+| `cold-evidence` status/count strip and panel 1 four capability rows | Preserve mock row labels/order: Temperature compliance (`TEMPERATURE_POINT` + `EXCURSION_BOUNDS`), Backup response (`BACKUP_START_RESULT` + `TRANSFER_POSITION`), Cooling recovery (`COOLING_RESPONSE`), Product exposure (`PRODUCT_EXPOSURE`). Map `SUPPORTED` to Full only for the precise claim; `LIMITED` to Limited, `UNAVAILABLE` to None, contradiction to Contradictory. Each row has Evidence chip, What we can say, What is missing and Next step. No single score or equipment health inference. Nonexistent Action/create destination is text, not a button. |
+| `cold-evidence` panel 2 six source rows | Preserve Chamber probe, inverter/battery, generator controller, compressor meter, transfer switch, loading/door log in mock order, using configured `cold-roles-1` identities and bound accepted source IDs. Do not collapse the two inverter/battery channels into an invented combined observed device: state each observed channel or `No source-specific record`. Columns remain Source, Status at window end, Quality, What it provides, Affects. Status is diagnostic contact (`RECENT/STALE/NEVER/UNKNOWN` mapped to plain words); Quality uses accepted evidence quality/coverage; fact age is separately labelled. Missing transfer and loading sources say `Not connected` only if configuration proves that; otherwise `No record`. |
+| `cold-evidence` panel 3 next-evidence rows and footer | Name missing roles in capability order, with why each constrains a claim and an exact Evidence or Financials destination. Witnessed test, loading log and stock are requirements/gaps, not accepted Actions. Footer links to this route's Analysis selection; no create Action button without a real managed Action. |
+| `cold-evidence-analysis` strip and panel 1 table | Preserve columns `Source · reading`, `Value`, `Measured (EAT)`, `Sent`, `Received`, `Record`. Existing response must therefore add `value_display`, `unit`, `sent_at` (nullable) and `record_target_state` to each accepted record; read value from accepted public payload only, never private truth. `source_time` is Measured, source publication time is Sent only if stored, `received_at` is Received. Convert EAT in UI while retaining UTC selection. Show exact ID/quality/exclusion and source provenance in row detail. A missing record appears as an explicit gap row only if W2-03 expectation/gap data proves it; it is never counted as accepted. Unproved record target follows retro ruling: labelled ID plus unavailable gap. |
+| `cold-evidence-analysis` panel 2 | `claim_rules` derived from selected W2-03 basis: requirement, observed/missing roles, coverage, result/limit, exact accepted IDs, source class and cutoff. State that source reporting is separate from equipment condition. Empty selection says `Claim limit not assessed`. No generic prose that implies a Full claim for uncovered periods. |
+| `cold-financials` status/count strip and four metric cards | Count actual returned consequence rows, not mock `2`; do not show USD in status. Cards stay Product exposure (bounded/unpriced or unassessed), Confirmed loss (`Unknown`), Repair and call-out (`Not recorded`), Verified savings (`No verified outcome recorded`). They do not turn missing evidence into numeric zero or verified savings. |
+| `cold-financials` panel 1 six-column bridge | Preserve `What happened`, `How it becomes money`, `Consequence`, `Basis`, `Confidence`, `Source`. One row for exact `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` when warranted; its quantity is bounded `degC_min`, translation is `Stock value × product response rule — not declared`, consequence `Not priced`, basis `Unpriced`, confidence from exact W2-03 claim, source exact Finding. A second backup/repair row is an **unpriced explanatory gap**, not a `RECORDED_REPAIR` DB row; label `No accepted repair cost record` and exact start Finding if one exists. If neither exists, show one in-table empty state, not two fictional rows. |
+| `cold-financials` panel 2 and panel 3 | Panel 2 shows the three reserved missing inputs: stock quantity/identity, unit value and product exposure rule; currency says `Not declared`, never USD. Button opens selected read-only assumptions. Panel 3 lists `not_included` reasons: spoilage/loss unproved, repair cost absent, source gate withheld when applicable. No current amount or portfolio roll-up link without an actual cold roll-up contract. |
+| `cold-assumptions` one panel and notice | Preserve columns `Assumption`, `Value`, `Used for`; rows in mock order are Stock value in chamber, Product exposure rule, Currency. Map them to `cold_stock_quantity`/`cold_stock_unit_value`, `cold_exposure_rule`, and missing currency (a separate explicit gap, not a publishable key). All say `Not declared`; used-for explains possible future translation only. Notice says a reviewed future assumption publication could re-translate the same evidence; no edit control or existing diesel POST. |
+
+The backend `/evidence/analysis` contract above is extended by `value_display:string|null`, `unit:string|null`, `sent_at:UTC|null`, and `record_target_state:PROVED|UNAVAILABLE` per accepted row. These are read-only public evidence fields, not a new accepted-evidence writer. The `/financial-assumptions` response adds `currency_state:'UNDECLARED'` and keeps `missing_keys` as the three reserved future keys; it must never return a fake USD item. The overview response includes card-specific `cooling` and `maintenance` capability gaps so the UI need not infer from absence. Test each field against a real selected replay and a valid empty window.
+
+All five routes keep the mock's local Site tabs, selected date bar, and `?view=analysis` behavior. A tab with no supported destination remains visible but disabled with its gap; no link navigates to a misleading mini-grid view. The five routes require all indexed panels in exact DOM order, panel title and grid; status/cards/notices above are separately asserted. Retro correction 14 is proved by an integration test or rendered as the exact-ID gap above; record which alternative shipped in the review packet. Both retro corrections are a separate frontend commit with the five-screen work and affected captures.

+## Final Architect rulings on commit 1 (Q1-Q10)
+
+This section overrides shorthand above where the two differ. Migration `0023` on `task/W2-04-cold-site-evidence-money` is provisional until its listed corrections are made; passing migration tests do not waive them.
+
+1. **Q1 CHANGE — selected window and room.** Keep `assessment_revision.window_start/window_end` for each trigger; neither episode bounds nor `window_assessment_revision_id` is a money window. For an `EXCURSION`, `cold_episode.asset_id` is the room. For a `START_RESPONSE`, that asset and the start Finding's `subject_asset_id` are the **generator**, so they must never fill `room_asset_id` or the overlap key. Resolve the effective chamber room from the exact assessment's selected `cold-roles-1` Foundation/binding basis (the same room that its temperature policy names); require one scoped room, or raise and roll back. Apply this to the Finding trigger, episode trigger, source-gate trigger and backfill. Prove that excursion and start Finding for one assessment produce the same room/window/group and that no generator ID is stored as a room.
+2. **Q2 ACCEPT.** Source-gate link dirties latest cold episode heads citing the batch through `cold_episode_evidence` and latest cold Finding heads citing it through `finding_revision_evidence`. Scope every subquery by tenant and Site as well as parent ID; distinct heads may produce distinct work rows but identical selected consequence basis returns the same revision.
+3. **Q3 CHANGE — preserve instant precision.** Canonical JSON array of strings and lowercase hyphenated UUIDs stand. Use UTC `Z` timestamps with six fractional digits **when microseconds are nonzero**, and whole seconds otherwise, matching canonical source instant formatting. Reject precision beyond microseconds at the API boundary; never truncate a selected window to seconds when hashing. The six replay windows happen to have whole seconds. SQL and Python golden vectors must match for whole-second and fractional inputs.
+4. **Q4 ACCEPT with meaning.** `limitation IS NOT NULL` means a named technical limit, not an arbitrary note. For an open W2-03 excursion with lower `degC_min` and null upper, keep `UNPRICED`, store that partial bound and a nonempty `OPEN_EPISODE_UPPER_UNKNOWN` limitation. `WITHHELD` may have either/both null bounds. A sound closed exposure has both. Null amount/currency remains mandatory in every case.
+5. **Q5 ACCEPT each extra.** Keep scoped self-FK, first-revision/prior-link CHECK, kind/model pairing, nonnull scoped room FK on revision and work, and nullable evidence-link reason. Add a same-Finding/kind predecessor check in store logic; FK alone does not prove chain membership. A successful no-Finding episode gets a no-op attempt, not a fabricated Finding or consequence.
+6. **Q6 ACCEPT.** The listed `DEFAULT clock_timestamp()` columns, including evidence-link `created_at`, are server times. The sole store writer must omit them, and tests must assert they lie in the DB transaction's observed interval. Do not accept client timestamps in store methods. A forcing trigger is unnecessary for this owner-restricted append-only table.
+7. **Q7 CHANGE — constrain the aggregate.** `source_classification` is exactly `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION|REGISTERED_SIMULATION|MIXED|UNKNOWN`, the values returned by `evidence/source_provenance.py`; add a SQL CHECK. `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` is not a current class. Preserve reason/detail and exact IDs separately; `UNKNOWN` and `MIXED` withhold money.
+8. **Q8 ACCEPT.** Missing room or invalid assessment window is an invariant violation: raise and roll back the W2-03 assessment/Finding transaction, following `0012` rather than silently dropping dirty work. The worker reports `FAILED` for later derivation faults; it cannot repair a missing enqueue.
+9. **Q9 ACCEPT with a nonvacuous guard.** Rule 8 scans runtime modules, so inspect the exact `0023` migration separately. Require at least one `INSERT INTO cold_consequence_work`, and require every such INSERT to use `ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, site_id, dirty_key_sha256) DO NOTHING`; reject `DO UPDATE`, another arbiter or a missing INSERT. Probe by mutating a temporary copy of the migration and restoring it, with pass and fail cases; no probe may pass solely because migrations are skipped by the runtime scan.
+10. **Q10 ACCEPT staged enforcement.** Commit 1 installs table/owner/probe-name registration and refuses foreign and mutating writes. Commit 2 adds real store INSERTs and enables per-table `inserts seen` assertions for revision, evidence and attempt. Before commit 2 is declared done, all three must be nonvacuously seen; no empty/fake SQL is allowed to satisfy the guard. The trigger-only work check is active in commit 1.
+
+## Implementer dry-read: exact backend behavior
+
+| Question a builder would otherwise decide | Binding answer and proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| Which Finding gets an exposure revision? | Only a selected, warranted `EXCURSION` FindingRevision with an exact `claim.episode_revision_id` and eligible episode. `START_RESPONSE` is chronology/context; do not emit the optional context consequence in W2-04. A successful start, unsupported excursion, or missing-probe episode without a Finding produces no consequence and may close its trigger work with an empty-ID success attempt. This avoids a second counted exposure and an invented zero row. |
+| Which assessment is exact? | Join FindingRevision `assessment_revision_id`, `claim.episode_revision_id`, episode revision `assessment_revision_id`, and the selected W2-03 assessment pair by scoped IDs. A mismatch is a typed worker failure `COLD_BASIS_MISMATCH`, never a latest-row fallback. For a source-gate event, re-read the latest settled heads at its transaction snapshot and enqueue only heads whose exact evidence IDs use the linked batch. |
+| How is `degC_min` computed? | Copy the selected excursion revision's `degree_minutes_low/high` as decimal strings after checking policy, coverage and evidence eligibility. Do not integrate an interpolated chart, derive from duration alone or use private trajectory. Preserve a proved lower bound with unknown upper as in Q4. `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED` has no exposure row; no episode means `UNKNOWN` summary if the selection is otherwise incomplete. |
+| How does revision identity work? | Hash the sorted exact accepted IDs with their SUPPORT/CONTEXT/EXCLUDED uses and reasons, Finding/episode/assessment UUIDs and basis hashes, selected window/cutoff, `cold-exposure/1`, quantity bounds, aggregate source class/gate digest, and explicit missing cold assumption markers. Existing identical `(finding_revision_id,kind,basis_sha256)` returns its ID. A changed basis appends `revision_no=max+1` and `prior_revision_id` under the work advisory lock. Never overwrite; reversion to a previously used basis must remain audit-visible, so include the newly selected cutoff/revision ID in the hash. |
+| What exactly is pending and when does a work item finish? | Unfinished means no `SUCCEEDED` attempt. Include any relevant PENDING/LEASED/FAILED W2-03 cold assessment job and any cold consequence work with no success; a failed attempt does not clear pending. Relevant means same tenant/Site, overlapping half-open selected window and room. Select work in `(enqueued_at,id)` order, lock its UUID, and use `retry_due_at` from the last attempt; no row status UPDATE. An episode-only no-op success has `[]` IDs. |
+| Which source/cutoff gates apply? | Resolve S4-05 aggregate provenance from the exact evidence IDs, not Site mode or a Lab manifest. Recompute after a new publication link. Return historical technical rows at an older explicit `known_at` with the historical label; never call their amount current. Closed disclosure gives 403 before returning restricted accepted IDs; mixed/unknown classification and pending/stale currentness withhold a current money claim. No cold price exists even with coherent simulation. |
+| What does `RECORDED_REPAIR` mean here? | It is schema reserved only. Neither a W2-03 start Finding nor mock WR-019 is an accepted, costed work record. No `RECORDED_REPAIR` row, repair amount, currency, incident or maintenance item is synthesized in W2-04. |
+| How does the API keep one snapshot? | Authenticate and scope Site first. Open one repeatable-read transaction; capture latest committed S2 disposition watermark once, resolve paired window and optional `known_at`, then call W2-03 read/store, Foundation, source provenance and consequence read methods with the same connection. If W2-03 exposes only HTTP calls, add a caller-connection read method to its existing assessment adapter; never invoke loopback HTTP for composition or import a SQL driver into `cold_site_reads.py`. |
+| What is a valid explicit selection/cursor? | `from,to,known_at` are strict UTC instants; both window bounds or neither, `from<to`, at most 31 days, no unknown query keys. `known_at` cannot exceed the captured committed watermark for claims; a future cutoff is 422 `COLD_TIME_INVALID`. Cursor stores exact canonical selection/basis and `(source_time,evidence_id)`; a well-formed cursor for another selection or changed basis is 409. Page after the tuple, never by offset. Empty selected evidence is 200 `UNKNOWN`; unavailable/corrupt storage is 503, not empty. |
+| What do `as_of` and contact mean? | For an accepted record, `as_of=received_at` (with separate `source_time`); for assessment/episode/Finding, use immutable revision `created_at`; for consequence use its `created_at`; for a configured policy use effective revision time; for a missing value use selected `known_at` and a limitation. Source contact uses only an identified source-specific diagnostic at/before cutoff; otherwise `UNKNOWN`, even if a gateway is online. Accepted fact age is `to - latest qualifying source_time`, separate from contact. |
+| Where do exact links go? | Only emit a clickable target after an integration test proves the target route renders that exact ID with Site, window and cutoff preserved. For an unproved cold record or assessment target, display the exact ID as labelled text plus `Analysis view not available for this cold record`; Finding and Site Evidence links that pass their own route proof remain clickable. Never turn a mock Action, Incident, Maintenance or unbuilt Cooling destination into a link. |
+
+Use `cold_site_reads.py` as a pure composition service, and put SQL in the existing assessment/consequence adapters. `financials/cold_recompute.py` receives store protocols and no SQL driver. The five GETs are composed in `main.py`; do not change the mini-grid routers. Run architecture/dependency, workflow, writer guard and targeted import probes after each owning commit. The review packet states the exact migration SHA, W2-03 predecessor revision, replay manifest IDs, accepted IDs, selected revision IDs and test database name.
+

+## Final Architect rulings on commit 1 (Q1-Q10)
+
+This section overrides shorthand above where the two differ. Migration `0023` on `task/W2-04-cold-site-evidence-money` is provisional until its listed corrections are made; passing migration tests do not waive them.
+
+1. **Q1 CHANGE — selected window and room.** Keep `assessment_revision.window_start/window_end` for each trigger; neither episode bounds nor `window_assessment_revision_id` is a money window. For an `EXCURSION`, `cold_episode.asset_id` is the room. For a `START_RESPONSE`, that asset and the start Finding's `subject_asset_id` are the **generator**, so they must never fill `room_asset_id` or the overlap key. Resolve the effective chamber room from the exact assessment's selected `cold-roles-1` Foundation/binding basis (the same room that its temperature policy names); require one scoped room, or raise and roll back. Apply this to the Finding trigger, episode trigger, source-gate trigger and backfill. Prove that excursion and start Finding for one assessment produce the same room/window/group and that no generator ID is stored as a room.
+2. **Q2 ACCEPT.** Source-gate link dirties latest cold episode heads citing the batch through `cold_episode_evidence` and latest cold Finding heads citing it through `finding_revision_evidence`. Scope every subquery by tenant and Site as well as parent ID; distinct heads may produce distinct work rows but identical selected consequence basis returns the same revision.
+3. **Q3 CHANGE — preserve instant precision.** Canonical JSON array of strings and lowercase hyphenated UUIDs stand. Use UTC `Z` timestamps with six fractional digits **when microseconds are nonzero**, and whole seconds otherwise, matching canonical source instant formatting. Reject precision beyond microseconds at the API boundary; never truncate a selected window to seconds when hashing. The six replay windows happen to have whole seconds. SQL and Python golden vectors must match for whole-second and fractional inputs.
+4. **Q4 ACCEPT with meaning.** `limitation IS NOT NULL` means a named technical limit, not an arbitrary note. For an open W2-03 excursion with lower `degC_min` and null upper, keep `UNPRICED`, store that partial bound and a nonempty `OPEN_EPISODE_UPPER_UNKNOWN` limitation. `WITHHELD` may have either/both null bounds. A sound closed exposure has both. Null amount/currency remains mandatory in every case.
+5. **Q5 ACCEPT each extra.** Keep scoped self-FK, first-revision/prior-link CHECK, kind/model pairing, nonnull scoped room FK on revision and work, and nullable evidence-link reason. Add a same-Finding/kind predecessor check in store logic; FK alone does not prove chain membership. A successful no-Finding episode gets a no-op attempt, not a fabricated Finding or consequence.
+6. **Q6 ACCEPT.** The listed `DEFAULT clock_timestamp()` columns, including evidence-link `created_at`, are server times. The sole store writer must omit them, and tests must assert they lie in the DB transaction's observed interval. Do not accept client timestamps in store methods. A forcing trigger is unnecessary for this owner-restricted append-only table.
+7. **Q7 CHANGE — constrain the aggregate.** `source_classification` is exactly `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION|REGISTERED_SIMULATION|MIXED|UNKNOWN`, the values returned by `evidence/source_provenance.py`; add a SQL CHECK. `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` is not a current class. Preserve reason/detail and exact IDs separately; `UNKNOWN` and `MIXED` withhold money.
+8. **Q8 ACCEPT.** Missing room or invalid assessment window is an invariant violation: raise and roll back the W2-03 assessment/Finding transaction, following `0012` rather than silently dropping dirty work. The worker reports `FAILED` for later derivation faults; it cannot repair a missing enqueue.
+9. **Q9 ACCEPT with a nonvacuous guard.** Rule 8 scans runtime modules, so inspect the exact `0023` migration separately. Require at least one `INSERT INTO cold_consequence_work`, and require every such INSERT to use `ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, site_id, dirty_key_sha256) DO NOTHING`; reject `DO UPDATE`, another arbiter or a missing INSERT. Probe by mutating a temporary copy of the migration and restoring it, with pass and fail cases; no probe may pass solely because migrations are skipped by the runtime scan.
+10. **Q10 ACCEPT staged enforcement.** Commit 1 installs table/owner/probe-name registration and refuses foreign and mutating writes. Commit 2 adds real store INSERTs and enables per-table `inserts seen` assertions for revision, evidence and attempt. Before commit 2 is declared done, all three must be nonvacuously seen; no empty/fake SQL is allowed to satisfy the guard. The trigger-only work check is active in commit 1.
+
+## Implementer dry-read: exact backend behavior
+
+| Question a builder would otherwise decide | Binding answer and proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| Which Finding gets an exposure revision? | Only a selected, warranted `EXCURSION` FindingRevision with an exact `claim.episode_revision_id` and eligible episode. `START_RESPONSE` is chronology/context; do not emit the optional context consequence in W2-04. A successful start, unsupported excursion, or missing-probe episode without a Finding produces no consequence and may close its trigger work with an empty-ID success attempt. This avoids a second counted exposure and an invented zero row. |
+| Which assessment is exact? | Join FindingRevision `assessment_revision_id`, `claim.episode_revision_id`, episode revision `assessment_revision_id`, and the selected W2-03 assessment pair by scoped IDs. A mismatch is a typed worker failure `COLD_BASIS_MISMATCH`, never a latest-row fallback. For a source-gate event, re-read the latest settled heads at its transaction snapshot and enqueue only heads whose exact evidence IDs use the linked batch. |
+| How is `degC_min` computed? | Copy the selected excursion revision's `degree_minutes_low/high` as decimal strings after checking policy, coverage and evidence eligibility. Do not integrate an interpolated chart, derive from duration alone or use private trajectory. Preserve a proved lower bound with unknown upper as in Q4. `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED` has no exposure row; no episode means `UNKNOWN` summary if the selection is otherwise incomplete. |
+| How does revision identity work? | Hash the sorted exact accepted IDs with their SUPPORT/CONTEXT/EXCLUDED uses and reasons, Finding/episode/assessment UUIDs and basis hashes, selected window/cutoff, `cold-exposure/1`, quantity bounds, aggregate source class/gate digest, and explicit missing cold assumption markers. Existing identical `(finding_revision_id,kind,basis_sha256)` returns its ID. A changed basis appends `revision_no=max+1` and `prior_revision_id` under the work advisory lock. Never overwrite; reversion to a previously used basis must remain audit-visible, so include the newly selected cutoff/revision ID in the hash. |
+| What exactly is pending and when does a work item finish? | Unfinished means no `SUCCEEDED` attempt. Include any relevant PENDING/LEASED/FAILED W2-03 cold assessment job and any cold consequence work with no success; a failed attempt does not clear pending. Relevant means same tenant/Site, overlapping half-open selected window and room. Select work in `(enqueued_at,id)` order, lock its UUID, and use `retry_due_at` from the last attempt; no row status UPDATE. An episode-only no-op success has `[]` IDs. |
+| Which source/cutoff gates apply? | Resolve S4-05 aggregate provenance from the exact evidence IDs, not Site mode or a Lab manifest. Recompute after a new publication link. Return historical technical rows at an older explicit `known_at` with the historical label; never call their amount current. Closed disclosure gives 403 before returning restricted accepted IDs; mixed/unknown classification and pending/stale currentness withhold a current money claim. No cold price exists even with coherent simulation. |
+| What does `RECORDED_REPAIR` mean here? | It is schema reserved only. Neither a W2-03 start Finding nor mock WR-019 is an accepted, costed work record. No `RECORDED_REPAIR` row, repair amount, currency, incident or maintenance item is synthesized in W2-04. |
+| How does the API keep one snapshot? | Authenticate and scope Site first. Open one repeatable-read transaction; capture latest committed S2 disposition watermark once, resolve paired window and optional `known_at`, then call W2-03 read/store, Foundation, source provenance and consequence read methods with the same connection. If W2-03 exposes only HTTP calls, add a caller-connection read method to its existing assessment adapter; never invoke loopback HTTP for composition or import a SQL driver into `cold_site_reads.py`. |
+| What is a valid explicit selection/cursor? | `from,to,known_at` are strict UTC instants; both window bounds or neither, `from<to`, at most 31 days, no unknown query keys. `known_at` cannot exceed the captured committed watermark for claims; a future cutoff is 422 `COLD_TIME_INVALID`. Cursor stores exact canonical selection/basis and `(source_time,evidence_id)`; a well-formed cursor for another selection or changed basis is 409. Page after the tuple, never by offset. Empty selected evidence is 200 `UNKNOWN`; unavailable/corrupt storage is 503, not empty. |
+| What do `as_of` and contact mean? | For an accepted record, `as_of=received_at` (with separate `source_time`); for assessment/episode/Finding, use immutable revision `created_at`; for consequence use its `created_at`; for a configured policy use effective revision time; for a missing value use selected `known_at` and a limitation. Source contact uses only an identified source-specific diagnostic at/before cutoff; otherwise `UNKNOWN`, even if a gateway is online. Accepted fact age is `to - latest qualifying source_time`, separate from contact. |
+| Where do exact links go? | Only emit a clickable target after an integration test proves the target route renders that exact ID with Site, window and cutoff preserved. For an unproved cold record or assessment target, display the exact ID as labelled text plus `Analysis view not available for this cold record`; Finding and Site Evidence links that pass their own route proof remain clickable. Never turn a mock Action, Incident, Maintenance or unbuilt Cooling destination into a link. |
+
+Use `cold_site_reads.py` as a pure composition service, and put SQL in the existing assessment/consequence adapters. `financials/cold_recompute.py` receives store protocols and no SQL driver. The five GETs are composed in `main.py`; do not change the mini-grid routers. Run architecture/dependency, workflow, writer guard and targeted import probes after each owning commit. The review packet states the exact migration SHA, W2-03 predecessor revision, replay manifest IDs, accepted IDs, selected revision IDs and test database name.
+

The status strip, objective, metric cards, tabs, notice and chips sit **outside** indexed panels; they still need truthful sources. Use the shared W1-F1 frame, grid and tokens. Before commit 2 is declared done, all three must be nonvacuously seen; no empty/fake SQL is allowed to satisfy the guard. The trigger-only work check is active in commit 1.
+
+## Implementer dry-read: exact backend behavior
+
+| Question a builder would otherwise decide | Binding answer and proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| Which Finding gets an exposure revision? | Only a selected, warranted `EXCURSION` FindingRevision with an exact `claim.episode_revision_id` and eligible episode. `START_RESPONSE` is chronology/context; do not emit the optional context consequence in W2-04. A successful start, unsupported excursion, or missing-probe episode without a Finding produces no consequence and may close its trigger work with an empty-ID success attempt. This avoids a second counted exposure and an invented zero row. |
+| Which assessment is exact? | Join FindingRevision `assessment_revision_id`, `claim.episode_revision_id`, episode revision `assessment_revision_id`, and the selected W2-03 assessment pair by scoped IDs. A mismatch is a typed worker failure `COLD_BASIS_MISMATCH`, never a latest-row fallback. For a source-gate event, re-read the latest settled heads at its transaction snapshot and enqueue only heads whose exact evidence IDs use the linked batch. |
+| How is `degC_min` computed? | Copy the selected excursion revision's `degree_minutes_low/high` as decimal strings after checking policy, coverage and evidence eligibility. Do not integrate an interpolated chart, derive from duration alone or use private trajectory. Preserve a proved lower bound with unknown upper as in Q4. `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED` has no exposure row; no episode means `UNKNOWN` summary if the selection is otherwise incomplete. |
+| How does revision identity work? | Hash the sorted exact accepted IDs with their SUPPORT/CONTEXT/EXCLUDED uses and reasons, Finding/episode/assessment UUIDs and basis hashes, selected window/cutoff, `cold-exposure/1`, quantity bounds, aggregate source class/gate digest, and explicit missing cold assumption markers. Existing identical `(finding_revision_id,kind,basis_sha256)` returns its ID. A changed basis appends `revision_no=max+1` and `prior_revision_id` under the work advisory lock. Never overwrite; reversion to a previously used basis must remain audit-visible, so include the newly selected cutoff/revision ID in the hash. |
+| What exactly is pending and when does a work item finish? | Unfinished means no `SUCCEEDED` attempt. Include any relevant PENDING/LEASED/FAILED W2-03 cold assessment job and any cold consequence work with no success; a failed attempt does not clear pending. Relevant means same tenant/Site, overlapping half-open selected window and room. Select work in `(enqueued_at,id)` order, lock its UUID, and use `retry_due_at` from the last attempt; no row status UPDATE. An episode-only no-op success has `[]` IDs. |
+| Which source/cutoff gates apply? | Resolve S4-05 aggregate provenance from the exact evidence IDs, not Site mode or a Lab manifest. Recompute after a new publication link. Return historical technical rows at an older explicit `known_at` with the historical label; never call their amount current. Closed disclosure gives 403 before returning restricted accepted IDs; mixed/unknown classification and pending/stale currentness withhold a current money claim. No cold price exists even with coherent simulation. |
+| What does `RECORDED_REPAIR` mean here? | It is schema reserved only. Neither a W2-03 start Finding nor mock WR-019 is an accepted, costed work record. No `RECORDED_REPAIR` row, repair amount, currency, incident or maintenance item is synthesized in W2-04. |
+| How does the API keep one snapshot? | Authenticate and scope Site first. Open one repeatable-read transaction; capture latest committed S2 disposition watermark once, resolve paired window and optional `known_at`, then call W2-03 read/store, Foundation, source provenance and consequence read methods with the same connection. If W2-03 exposes only HTTP calls, add a caller-connection read method to its existing assessment adapter; never invoke loopback HTTP for composition or import a SQL driver into `cold_site_reads.py`. |
+| What is a valid explicit selection/cursor? | `from,to,known_at` are strict UTC instants; both window bounds or neither, `from<to`, at most 31 days, no unknown query keys. `known_at` cannot exceed the captured committed watermark for claims; a future cutoff is 422 `COLD_TIME_INVALID`. Cursor stores exact canonical selection/basis and `(source_time,evidence_id)`; a well-formed cursor for another selection or changed basis is 409. Page after the tuple, never by offset. Empty selected evidence is 200 `UNKNOWN`; unavailable/corrupt storage is 503, not empty. |
+| What do `as_of` and contact mean? | For an accepted record, `as_of=received_at` (with separate `source_time`); for assessment/episode/Finding, use immutable revision `created_at`; for consequence use its `created_at`; for a configured policy use effective revision time; for a missing value use selected `known_at` and a limitation. Source contact uses only an identified source-specific diagnostic at/before cutoff; otherwise `UNKNOWN`, even if a gateway is online. Accepted fact age is `to - latest qualifying source_time`, separate from contact. |
+| Where do exact links go? | Only emit a clickable target after an integration test proves the target route renders that exact ID with Site, window and cutoff preserved. For an unproved cold record or assessment target, display the exact ID as labelled text plus `Analysis view not available for this cold record`; Finding and Site Evidence links that pass their own route proof remain clickable. Never turn a mock Action, Incident, Maintenance or unbuilt Cooling destination into a link. |
+
+Use `cold_site_reads.py` as a pure composition service, and put SQL in the existing assessment/consequence adapters. `financials/cold_recompute.py` receives store protocols and no SQL driver. The five GETs are composed in `main.py`; do not change the mini-grid routers. Run architecture/dependency, workflow, writer guard and targeted import probes after each owning commit. The review packet states the exact migration SHA, W2-03 predecessor revision, replay manifest IDs, accepted IDs, selected revision IDs and test database name.
